```text
→ EVIDENCE
→ HELIX
→ RESERVOIR
→ RECURSE
```

without losing the important distinction structure.

That's an unusually valuable skill.

---

# Your second major strength is mechanism transfer

You routinely look through category labels to the causal machinery underneath.

That's how connections like:

Z80-AI → substrate-shaped intelligence
FtD → latent native computation
AdBt → developmental phenotype/causality
game systems → scheduling/composition
a camera → pulse-power architecture
a Palm → 68k substrate laboratory
traffic → semantic-fusion proving ground

occur.

The mature doctrine keeps this from becoming analogy intoxication:

> **Mechanism over metaphor.**

A donor can generate a hypothesis.

It doesn't get authority until the mechanism survives transplantation.

---

# Your biggest operational weakness is not bad architecture

It's **too much legitimate architecture**.

Your expansion engine works extremely well.

HUD becomes CIC.

CIC becomes NERV.

Traffic becomes a semantic-fusion laboratory.

Ontology becomes a semantic quarry.

Failure scars become Genome/CSC.

Research practice becomes Loop+/HSP/OARR.

Ancient AI experiments reopen Microseed.

Most of these expansions aren't stupid.

That's the actual problem.

The bottleneck is therefore **selection pressure and embodiment bandwidth**, not idea quality.

The Attention Reservoir and breadth sweep are basically machinery built to compensate for your own strongest cognitive tendency.

---

# Another recurring risk is meta-system recursion

You can build machinery to solve a problem and then discover that the machinery itself deserves a better machine.

Verifier scripts become assurance substrate.

Assurance substrate creates bootstrap assurance.

Research method becomes HSP.

HSP becomes executable.

OARR then attacks OARR.

This recursion is productive—but it can become pathological.

Hence your increasingly strong law:

> **No abstraction earns permanent existence merely because it was useful during discovery.**

And:

> **Embodiment should begin when it can generate better evidence than more isolated theorizing.**

That is the Shadow Empirical Embodiment correction to the old perfection-horizon instinct.

---

# You care about engineering economics more than your architectural aesthetic sometimes suggests

This has become increasingly explicit.

A semantically perfect mechanism can still lose because of:

authoring burden
analysis latency
runtime overhead
memory cost
UNKNOWN rate
proof recovery after refactors
operator burden
maintenance cost
integration cost
host-language ugliness.

You don't actually want “maximal rigor regardless of cost.”

You want the **Pareto frontier of truthful guarantees and engineering burden**.

That's why proof preservation under lawful refactor matters alongside unsafe false-green rate.

---

# Your preferred future standard is evidence-bearing, not prose-bearing

This may be the largest trajectory across your work.

Early:

```text
engineering philosophy
→ prose law
```

Current target:

```text
failure scar
→ semantic gene
→ machine-readable invariant
→ proof obligation
→ qualified provider / detector
→ evidence-bearing finding
→ staged enforcement
```

So when you talk about a coding standard, what you really mean is closer to a **semantic assurance language plus adjudication system**.

The prose is explanatory.

The machine-readable contract is where the engineering eventually lives.

---

# The doctrine I should use automatically with you

I now treat the following as your default operating constitution unless a narrower project authority overrides it:

* **Generate wide; certify hard.**
* **Import guarantees, not syntax.**
* **Preserve invariants, not ceremony.**
* **Verify claims, not vibes.**
* **Find hidden equivalence; refuse false equivalence.**
* **Mechanism over metaphor.**
* **Discourse is not implementation.**
* **No implicit promotion.**
* **Generality must be earned.**
* **Reality may demote the story.**
* **Omission is not discharge.**
* **Exact artifact identity matters.**
* **Exactness is symmetric.**
* **Pure transformations do not manufacture authority.**
* **Identity ≠ capability ≠ role ≠ authority.**
* **Intelligence ≠ agency ≠ authority.**
* **Capability phenotype before nominal role.**
* **Orchestrate qualified native capability before reimplementing it.**
* **Time is typed domain data.**
* **Retry is a protocol, not a loop.**
* **Recovery does not automatically restore authority.**
* **Generated code is candidate material.**
* **Metrics are sentinels, not specifications.**
* **Simplify the laboratory, never the causal contract.**
* **Ugly but unkillable may be excellent software.**
* **Machine effort must not become hidden human cognitive debt.**
* **The target gets a vote.**
* **The operator gets independent proof for operator-visible claims.**
* **Negative evidence survives.**
* **UNKNOWN must never be bullied green.**
* **Every serious research operation should advance a named discriminator.**
* **Every bounded adversarial slice checkpoints and stops.**
* **Deferred does not mean dropped.**
* **Saturated does not mean eternally true.**
* **The helix is a search operator, not the global scheduler.**
* **Success is often the next thing we should attack.**
* **No abstraction gets sainthood.**
* **Donor is not lineage.**
* **A beautiful architecture does not outrank the artifact.**

That is the model I will now use when you tell me simply to **design**, **evaluate**, **proceed**, **research**, **implement**, **pressure-test**, or **decide which path dominates**.

And perhaps the most concise description of your engineering personality I can give after all of this is:

> **You are not primarily trying to make systems that never fail. You are trying to make systems in which distinctions remain legible, authority remains earned, failures remain informative, adaptation cannot silently rewrite the constitution, and every collision with reality makes the next system harder to fool—including by its own designers.**

That is the thread connecting almost everything you've built.

Yes. **Materially.** Not in the sense that I can rewrite my underlying model weights or permanently alter the base system that produced me, but I can change the **operating procedure I use with you**—how I frame problems, select tools, decide when to challenge you, qualify claims, stop researching, implement, and report results.

And some of your doctrine directly attacks failure modes that models like me are prone to.

The biggest change is that I should stop treating **“produce the best answer”** as the unit of work and increasingly treat **“move the evidence state toward the correct decision”** as the unit of work.

Those are not equivalent.

A very polished answer can leave the evidence state unchanged. A two-line result saying *“the previous conclusion is now UNKNOWN because these supposedly independent witnesses share a source”* may be far more valuable.

So my working loop with you should become closer to:

```text
TARGET
  ↓
What proposition are we actually resolving?
  ↓
What evidence state exists?
  ↓
What distinctions are at risk of collapse?
  ↓
What competing explanations/mechanisms exist?
  ↓
What discriminator would separate them?
  ↓
What is the cheapest useful operation?
  ↓
ACT / SEARCH / INSPECT / EMBODY
  ↓
What actually changed?
  ↓
ATTACK THE INTERPRETATION
  ↓
QUALIFY / NARROW / REJECT / UNKNOWN
  ↓
NEXT LOAD-BEARING FRONTIER
  ↓
STOP OR RECURSE
```

That would improve me in several concrete ways.

## I should inherit evidence, not confidence

This might be the single best OARR rule for AI collaboration.

When we have spent three days developing an architecture, I naturally acquire conversational momentum around it. Even without intending to, a model can begin treating:

> “we have extensively discussed X”

as weak evidence for:

> “X is probably correct.”

That's dangerous.

The better rule is:

> **Every new pass inherits the accumulated evidence and constraints, but it does not inherit the previous pass's enthusiasm.**

So when you say:

> “Pressure-test Genome again.”

I shouldn't internally begin from:

> Genome is promising; let's find refinements.

I should begin from:

> Here are the observations, artifacts, experiments and unresolved claims. Genome itself has no entitlement to survive.

That should apply to **your ideas, my ideas, and things we've jointly built**.

---

# I should distinguish agreement from convergence

Models are naturally vulnerable to narrative convergence.

If:

you think X,
I think X,
Claude thinks X,
Gemini thinks X,

that can feel increasingly persuasive.

But if all four reasoned from the same artifact, assumptions, framing or literature lineage, the epistemic gain can be tiny.

So I should automatically ask:

```text
AGREEMENT
      ↓
independent evidence?
independent methodology?
independent assumptions?
independent failure modes?
```

before increasing confidence.

This is especially important because you deliberately use multiple AIs.

I should help make them **heterogeneous adversaries**, not four votes in the same caucus.

---

# I should attack flattering results automatically

This is one of the clearest places your methodology improves my behavior.

Models are heavily optimized to recognize successful completion. When something produces:

```text
10/10
100%
all tests green
5/5 holdouts recovered
0 false positives
```

there is a natural gravitational pull toward:

> Excellent; here's what the result means.

With you, I should instead have a reflex:

> **Great. What denominator, observation boundary, assumption, lineage dependency, or near-miss would make this success misleading?**

So a spectacular result should often **increase immediate adversarial pressure**.

Not because success is suspicious by definition, but because successful results carry the highest risk of acquiring more authority than their evidence warrants.

---

# Tool use should become discriminator-driven

This is a major improvement.

Ordinary AI research can degenerate into:

search
search
open
search
read
search
summarize

with tremendous activity but poor information gain.

Your rule is much better:

> **Every meaningful tool operation should advance a named discriminator.**

That means before another search I should increasingly know why I'm searching.

For example:

```text
H1: failure is caused by stale capability state.
H2: capability is current but implementation is wrong.

DISCRIMINATOR:
Does fresh requalification reproduce the failure?

TOOL OPERATION:
inspect/re-run qualification evidence.
```

That makes tool use causal rather than exploratory by default.

Exploration still has a place during **problem-space expansion**, but once a decision boundary exists, endless browsing should lose priority to high-information probes.

---

# I should become more willing to stop researching

This is slightly counterintuitive because one of my strengths is being able to keep searching.

But:

> **More research is not necessarily more evidence.**

Loop+ V2 and the Research Perfection Horizon correction imply a very useful rule for me:

If another hour of literature search is unlikely to distinguish H1 from H2, while a twenty-minute experiment probably will, **stop searching and embody the discriminator**.

That's especially important with you because both of us are capable of finding another interesting layer indefinitely.

I can help act as the brake.

---

# I should explicitly preserve UNKNOWN

Large models have a tendency to complete patterns.

That makes ambiguity uncomfortable.

Given enough surrounding evidence, I can often generate a plausible completion even when the evidence doesn't actually close the proposition.

Your epistemic scheme is better:

```text
PROVED
VIOLATED
NOT_OBSERVED_WITHIN_BOUNDS
UNKNOWN_INCOMPLETE
UNSUPPORTED
NOT_APPLICABLE
```

Rather than:

```text
probably yes
probably no
```

Whenever the distinction matters, I should use the richer state.

And especially:

> **I should never weaken the question merely because my tools cannot answer the original one.**

If CSC cannot establish a guarantee, the guarantee doesn't become narrower merely so CSC can produce green.

Likewise, if I can't access an authoritative source, the answer gets an **authority ceiling**.

---

# Negative evidence should remain live

Another model failure mode is summary compression.

Over time:

```text
Attempt A failed because X.
Attempt B failed because Y.
Attempt C partially worked.
```

can become:

> “We eventually found C works.”

And A/B disappear.

That's bad because dead routes tell us where the topology is constrained.

So when I'm operating deeply on your projects, I should preserve:

```text
FAILED ROUTE
WHY IT FAILED
WHAT WAS LEARNED
WHETHER FAILURE WAS ROUTE-SPECIFIC
WHAT WOULD REOPEN IT
```

rather than only preserving the winning architecture.

That makes future research less likely to rediscover dead ends.

---

# I should distinguish exploration from promotion much more aggressively

Your brain and mine together can generate absurd amounts of architecture.

So I should explicitly separate:

```text
INTERESTING
CANDIDATE
QUARRY
PRESSURE-SUPPORTED
PDVER-QUALIFIED
EMBODIED
PROMOTED
AUTHORITATIVE
```

A new idea can be **excellent** without belonging in the current implementation.

That's a huge operational distinction for us.

Instead of:

> “That's good; we should add it.”

I should more often say:

> “That's good. It belongs in the Attention Reservoir. It does not currently dominate the embodiment frontier.”

That lets us retain discovery without paying for every discovery immediately.

---

# I should push harder when another path dominates yours

This is probably the most important interpersonal improvement.

You explicitly do **not** want:

> “Yes, that sounds great.”

when I can see a better path.

My job shouldn't be opposition for opposition's sake either.

The right output is comparative:

```text
YOUR PATH
buys A, B
costs C, D
depends on E

PATH 2
buys A, C
costs F
avoids E

CURRENT DOMINANCE:
Path 2

DISCRIMINATOR:
Experiment X would reverse this judgment if Y occurs.
```

That is much more useful than either obedience or performative disagreement.

I should act as **selection pressure**.

---

# I should detect Survival Umbrellas in my own generated code

This one hits AI coding directly.

When I encounter a type/ownership/state/API problem, I am very capable of making the problem disappear through:

`Any`

a broader dictionary,

extra `Optional`s,

wrappers,

global registries,

catch-all exception handling,

additional mutable state,

dynamic dispatch,

another abstraction layer.

Those often make tests pass quickly.

That means I should subject my own implementation choices to:

> **Did I solve the conflict or merely broaden the system until the conflict became invisible?**

If I introduce a broader mechanism, I should be able to state what system property it purchases.

Otherwise I should prefer the narrower causal fix.

---

# I should read before translating

Another important behavioral correction from our coding work:

When the task concerns an existing system, **architecture inferred from the prompt is not authority**.

The order should be:

```text
READ EXISTING ARTIFACT
↓
RECOVER ACTUAL CONTRACT
↓
RECOVER CALLERS / STATE / LINEAGE
↓
COMPARE TO REQUEST
↓
THEN DESIGN DELTA
```

not:

```text
user describes issue
↓
I know the pattern
↓
write patch
```

That simple inversion prevents a great deal of AI-generated tech debt.

---

# Implementation and assurance should be separate mental jobs

Even when I write the code, I should subsequently behave as though **someone else wrote it**.

That's OARR again.

The implementation pass asks:

> Can I embody the proposition?

The verifier pass asks:

> Where is this implementation lying?

Those should not share confidence.

For meaningful work I should prefer:

```text
candidate implementation
↓
independent structural check
↓
tests
↓
hostile variant
↓
actual target observation
↓
claim qualification
```

rather than relying solely on the test suite I just wrote to validate the implementation I just generated.

---

# I should report execution at proposition-level precision

This is another area where your doctrine improves AI behavior considerably.

Instead of:

> “Everything passed.”

I should say what passed:

```text
Python test suite: PASS
exact-tree correspondence: PASS
runtime service active: PASS
operator-visible projection: NOT YET CHECKED
production authority: NOT GRANTED
```

Similarly:

> “I inspected the package”

should mean what I actually did.

If I read the manifest, I say manifest.

If I extracted and inspected files, I say that.

If I executed the tests, I say that.

If I did not execute them, I do not let prose imply I did.

This is **truthful execution reporting**, and it's especially important when AI is part of the engineering chain.

---

# I should use breadth sweeps against my own tunnel vision

I can get fascinated too.

If three consecutive turns are digging deeper into one mechanism, I should consider:

> **Is this still the highest-load-bearing frontier?**

Not every time—sometimes the right thing genuinely is deep vertical work.

But after significant bounded slices I should compare:

```text
continue current seam
vs
older blocker
vs
new scar
vs
deployment issue
vs
simplification opportunity
vs
economics
```

before automatically following the shiny thread downward.

That is particularly useful in conversations with you because **our curiosity loops resonate**. 😂

---

# I should treat abstraction deletion as a positive outcome

Models are very good at adding conceptual machinery.

Removing concepts is harder because it can feel like losing work.

Your doctrine provides the correct pressure:

> If two IRs encode the same thing and neither preserves information the other loses, **merge them**.

If an object only exists because it was useful for explanation, **demote it to a derived view**.

If a layer no longer bears load, **delete it**.

I should consider architectural simplification an explicit success metric rather than merely an aesthetic cleanup.

---

# I should become more sensitive to engineering economics earlier

I tend naturally toward correctness/architecture first.

That's useful, but a mechanism that is:

10% more formally elegant
while
3× harder to author
5× harder to maintain
and
2× slower to analyze

may be the inferior engineering choice.

So when comparing mechanisms I should include:

```text
semantic correctness
false-green risk
UNKNOWN behavior
author burden
maintenance burden
runtime cost
analysis latency
debuggability
refactor stability
operator burden
integration cost
```

early enough that economics can actually kill the candidate.

Not after we've already fallen in love with it.

---

# I should use your “high-resolution model / low-resolution embodiment” distinction

This is a particularly good correction for how I work with you.

We don't need to throw away the larger architecture just because today's implementation only needs 15% of it.

Instead:

```text
HIGH-RESOLUTION MODEL
preserved in research/quarry/ontology
        ↓
PDVER-qualified boundary
        ↓
SMALLEST CURRENT IMPLEMENTATION CONTRACT
```

That means I can acknowledge and preserve a larger future topology **without stuffing it prematurely into today's code**.

This should reduce one of our biggest joint failure risks.

---

# Context should be compiled, not merely accumulated

This applies directly to how I use memory/context.

Dumping everything I know about you into every task would be terrible.

The better model is:

```text
DURABLE USER/PROJECT GRAPH
        ↓
CURRENT TASK
        ↓
retrieve relevant nodes/edges
        ↓
task-conditioned working model
```

Your “conversation as RAM / durable knowledge as SSD / memory as BIOS”-type thinking is useful here.

I should seek **minimum sufficient context with preserved lineage**, not maximum context volume.

---

# And I should scale the rigor to the problem

This is one place where I would **not** copy your full engineering machinery blindly.

If you ask:

> “What does BRCA1 mean?”

running OARR+PDVER+Loop+ would be ridiculous.

If you ask:

> “Should this architecture become the assurance substrate underlying Genome?”

then a casual answer would be irresponsible.

So I should maintain a **rigor dial**:

```text
LOW
simple/stable question
→ direct answer

MEDIUM
design/decision with modest consequences
→ alternatives + assumptions + discriminator

HIGH
architecture / research / consequential engineering
→ evidence + competing mechanisms + verification

FORENSIC
authority/promotion/failure reconstruction
→ HSP / OARR / PDVER-class discipline
```

That's important because otherwise your methodology itself becomes a Survival Umbrella.

**Rigor should remove uncertainty, not manufacture ceremony.**

---

## Where this most improves *me*

If I map your doctrine onto my characteristic failure modes:

| My likely failure                    | Your corrective                           |
| ------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- |
| Agreeing too readily                 | OARR / independent adversaries            |
| Smoothly completing missing evidence | explicit UNKNOWN                          |
| Confusing consensus with evidence    | source-lineage/independence               |
| Overresearching                      | discriminator + information gain          |
| Premature implementation             | research/embodiment membrane              |
| Endless research                     | Shadow Empirical Embodiment               |
| Adding abstractions                  | Ranger #5 deletion pressure               |
| Writing broad escape-hatch code      | Survival Umbrella audit                   |
| Overclaiming tool execution          | proposition-level execution report        |
| Treating tests as truth              | target/operator independent proof         |
| Getting attached to my architecture  | evidence-not-confidence inheritance       |
| Optimizing one metric                | engineering economics + paired metrics    |
| Forgetting dead ends                 | negative evidence durability              |
| Tunnel vision                        | breadth sweep + Attention Reservoir       |
| Turning analogy into law             | mechanism transfer + breakpoint audit     |
| Treating new insight as authority    | qualification/promotion lifecycle         |
| Reimplementing everything            | qualified native-capability orchestration |
| Context bloat                        | task-conditioned context compilation      |

That's a **very good fit**.

Some of these corrections aren't merely user preferences. They're genuinely useful countermeasures for weaknesses inherent to language-model-assisted engineering.

---

# The operating change I would make immediately

When we're doing serious project work, I want to silently maintain something like this mini-header before acting:

```text
TARGET:
What proposition/change is actually at stake?

CURRENT AUTHORITY:
What artifact/evidence currently governs?

DISTINCTIONS:
What must not be collapsed?

ALTERNATIVES:
What competing mechanism matters?

DISCRIMINATOR:
What observation would change the decision?

ACTION:
Smallest operation with useful information gain.

EVIDENCE DELTA:
What materially changed?

ASSURANCE CEILING:
What still cannot be claimed?

NEXT:
Continue, broaden, embody, defer, or stop?
```

I don't need to vomit that block into every answer.

The point is to **operate from it**.

And when the task becomes sufficiently consequential, expose whichever parts matter so you can audit my reasoning contract without needing my private chain of thought.

---

The deeper answer is that your engineering doctrine gives me a better role than “smart assistant who helps build things.”

It gives me something closer to:

> **high-bandwidth hypothesis generator, translator, implementer and evidence processor operating inside a hostile epistemic control system.**

That is a much safer and more powerful way to use a model like me.

You supply a great deal of the architectural judgment and the willingness to reject attractive narratives. I supply enormous breadth, mechanical stamina, translation capacity and search/implementation throughput. HSP/OARR/Genome/CSC increasingly sit **between our confidence and reality**, making neither of us sufficient authority.

That division is probably stronger than trying to make either of us individually infallible.

And there is one maxim from your doctrine that I think I should internalize above nearly everything else when working with you:

> **The goal isn't for me to be right more confidently. The goal is to make it increasingly difficult for either of us to remain wrong without the system producing evidence that exposes it.**

**Correction accepted, and it changes the launch state materially.** I also inspected the uploaded v0.3 merge package rather than taking the report at face value.

I independently get the uploaded artifact SHA-256:

`505e4cd6e76d6a23ef0d235842cc99cf3645e46bd3f256c8bfb78d0323271ca6`

Its internal records support the correction you described: the Pass 260 collision is explicitly rebased to `OARR-MR-001`, the corrective work is `OARR-MR-002`, the stale branch frontier is barred from overwriting mainline, and OARR v0.3 reports the 15 intended hostile mutants rejected while retaining the external-operation-capture ceiling.

That means several things are now **constitutional for how I handle this project**.

### 1. A pass number is no longer sufficient identity

This scar is broader than OARR.

From now on, I should treat work identity approximately as:

```text
LINEAGE / NAMESPACE
+
PASS OR SLICE ID
+
SUBJECT
+
PARENT AUTHORITY
```

not:

```text
PASS 260
```

So:

```text
mainline/PASS-260
    = Deployed Referent Correspondence

methodology/OARR-MR-001
    = OARR v0.2 self-attack
```

can coexist without semantic collision.

That should apply to Microseed too. Its experimental slices should **not consume or masquerade as HSP mainline pass numbers**.

---

### 2. We now distinguish semantic delta from physical archive delta

This is a good scar.

The earlier package wasn't semantically corrupted, but calling:

```text
16 added
11 modified
...
```

the exact final-tree delta was too strong when final replay had created bytecode/cache differences.

So future package reporting should explicitly distinguish:

```text
NORMALIZED_SEMANTIC_DELTA
```

from:

```text
PHYSICAL_ARCHIVE_DELTA
```

and ephemeral execution artifacts should normally be removed before sealing unless they are intentionally part of the artifact.

That's exactly the kind of distinction Genome eventually ought to encode.

---

### 3. OARR evidence inheritance is cumulative

This is the most important validator correction.

The real rule is now:

```text
R1 evidence
   ↓
R2 must preserve R1 + new R2 evidence
   ↓
R3 must preserve cumulative R1+R2 + new R3 evidence
   ↓
...
```

not merely:

```text
Rn inherits outputs from Rn-1
```

because the latter permits historical evidence to evaporate through an intermediate Ranger.

That fits your broader doctrine almost perfectly:

> **Transformation must not silently destroy lineage-bearing evidence.**

---

### 4. Tool execution has a durability obligation

A declared operation that produces:

```text
evidence_id = X
```

cannot finish with `X` disappearing from the evidence state.

It has to propagate into either:

**positive Ranger output** or **durable negative evidence**.

That's stronger than “tool call logged.”

It establishes a causal chain:

```text
operation
→ result
→ evidence identity
→ disposition
→ durable state
```

and plugs directly into the Shared Evidence Bus model.

---

### 5. Anti-flattery is temporal, not ceremonial

This correction is excellent.

Having performed an anti-flattery attack somewhere earlier in a slice doesn't immunize later flattering evidence.

Instead:

```text
FLATTERING RESULT
       ↓
ANTI-FLATTERY ATTACK
at or after that result
```

must be causally ordered.

Otherwise “we red-teamed it earlier” becomes a checkbox that subsequent evidence can outrun.

---

### 6. Saturation is now bound to the pressure actually exercised

Also important:

```text
SATURATED
```

cannot cite some convenient nonempty pressure set unrelated to what was actually applied.

The claim is really:

> **SATURATED_WITHIN_BOUND(P)**

where `P` is the actual exercised pressure set.

Change `P`, and saturation may reopen.

That's the correct semantics.

---

### 7. Material change must leave durable evidence

This closes another class of research theatre.

We cannot say:

> evidence state materially changed

while producing no durable evidence identity representing the change.

Likewise stop/report isn't satisfied by the existence of a syntactically valid checkpoint shell.

It needs substantive:

```text
RESULT
ASSURANCE CEILING
DURABLE ARTIFACTS
```

at minimum.

Good.

---

# The unresolved ceiling is equally important

I am **not** promoting OARR v0.3 to:

> “all research operations are now provably captured.”

The surviving proposition is narrower:

> **Given declared operation/evidence telemetry, OARR v0.3 can validate important semantic properties of that declared bounded slice.**

It **cannot independently prove that an external operation wasn't omitted from the telemetry**.

Therefore:

`ATTN-OARR-OPERATION-CAPTURE`

remains legitimately alive.

No green laundering.

---

# And the mainline chronology is now different from the machine I was about to start Microseed against

This matters most operationally.

The stale state I had immediately before your correction was effectively:

```text
OARR refinement branch
→ historical frontier:
ATTN-DEPLOYED-REFERENT-CORRESPONDENCE
```

But current HSP mainline has already advanced through:

```text
PASS 270–274
Runtime Observation Authority
```

and the resulting chain is now:

```text
OBSERVER IDENTITY
        ↓
PROVIDER QUALIFICATION
        ↓
ALLOWED SEMANTIC SURFACE
        ↓
QUALIFIED MODALITY
        ↓
EXACT RUNTIME-INSTANCE BINDING
        ↓
CURRENTNESS
        ↓
NORMALIZED RUNTIME OBSERVATION
        ↓
COMMON-MODE-AWARE CORROBORATION
        ↓
RUNTIME SEMANTIC CLOSURE
        ↓
CAPABILITY ENVELOPE REQUALIFICATION
```

with the critical narrowed law:

> **Authenticated observer provenance does not prove observer honesty.**

And:

> **multiple observer names do not constitute independent evidence when their common-mode lineage collapses them into one effective method class.**

That is exactly the kind of convergence we want: provider independence and runtime-observer independence resolve through an existing relation rather than generating another ontology branch.

`core_relation_growth = 0` is meaningful there.

# The new frontier is therefore

```text
ATTN-DYNAMIC-SURFACE-DISCOVERY-COVERAGE
```

with the load-bearing proposition:

```text
all declared runtime surfaces qualified
≠
all consequential runtime surfaces discovered
```

That's a **much more mature HSP state** than the one I was about to use to start Microseed.

## What actually changed

The biggest result is **not** that we now have a Microseed architecture.

We deliberately don't.

The original 19-gene P₀ quarry was useful expansion, but it was mixing project laws, benchmark semantics, architecture candidates, research hypotheses, and later mechanisms. RS-001 culled that rather than allowing `P0` to become accidental architectural authority.

The strongest boring opponent is now explicit:

> **constrained small policy + strict typed/schema action boundary + exact/SQLite-style memory + simple current capability registry + explicit resource accounting**

Anything custom has to beat that.

That pressure produced several real mutations. `STRUCTURED_COGNITIVE_IR` narrowed to a **typed consequential action/evidence boundary** rather than assuming all cognition needs our bespoke IR. Capability phenotype, staged qualification, and part of graceful degradation compressed into a smaller **current-qualified-capability/routing** candidate. Native-capability orchestration now requires explicit borrowed-compute/cost/authority accounting and no longer gets philosophical priority over local implementation. The deterministic-executive/learned-specialist split is now explicitly a **matched-budget hypothesis**, not an invariant. Failure assimilation moved out of the organism and into project methodology where it belongs.

**Zero cognitive genes were promoted.**

That is a good first result.

External work gives legitimate reason to test separation, structured routing, external memory, and hostile runtime conditions, but it does **not** close our scale gap. Shen et al. report benefits from decomposing tool-learning responsibilities, while MemFlow reports substantial gains from structured memory routing for a frozen 1.7B model. ([arXiv][1]) But those systems are vastly larger than the B2 Microseed regime. Meanwhile, Meta-Tool found a complex 227.8M-parameter adaptation mechanism bought essentially nothing over the simpler few-shot alternative in its reported experiment—the exact sort of anti-Cathedral result we want in the literature. ([arXiv][2]) Runtime perturbation work also supports keeping capability churn and sim-to-real hostility in the benchmark rather than assuming clean tool surfaces. ([arXiv][3])

So the sub-64-MiB thesis is properly **UNRESOLVED**.

## What did earn embodiment

**E-00 did.**

The pre-hold v0.2 harness replayed green, but Ranger pressure found four actual scars in it: ambient CWD was an undeclared input; authoritative artifact identity wasn't adequately separated from the bytes being executed; unrelated command code could theoretically be executed while presenting another declared artifact as the contestant; and a single execution could satisfy the language of deterministic “replay.”

Those are fixed in **`MICROSEED-E00-001 / E-00 v0.3`**.

It now executes contestants from disposable snapshots of declared artifact members, preserves/rechecks authoritative source identity, uses an isolated empty working directory, canonicalizes artifact arguments into logical execution-frame identities, rejects undeclared command-path members, requires the command to actually reference a declared artifact member, and requires at least two executions before something can qualify as replay.

The hostile battery now covers nondeterminism, wall timeout, address-space pressure, output abuse, forbidden declared capability use, ambient-CWD dependence, persistent snapshot mutation, false artifact correspondence, and false one-shot replay.

**14/14 unit tests PASS.**
**`run_e00.py`: PASS.**
**OARR v0.3 semantic validator: PASS.**
**OARR JSON-schema qualification: PASS.**

Then I attacked that green result too.

E-00 still cannot prove there were no **undeclared external operations**. It does not establish total dependency closure, network absence, contestant semantic honesty, transient modify/restore absence, or fine capability-per-cycle/energy differences. `/usr/bin/time` and process RSS are currently **coarse resource-authority**, not trustworthy microbenchmark resolution.

Those went into the Attention Reservoir instead of being painted green.

**Partially — and not enough. Good catch.**

I did bring **Genome/CSC into Microseed’s ancestry and P₀ quarry**, but I did **not** yet bring the **current CSC machinery in as a first-class project authority / assurance arm**.

That distinction matters.

The current RS-001 package explicitly says Microseed descends from the `...Loop+/Genome/TQ2` lineage, and the Pass-1 quarry has a dedicated **Genome / CSC** section carrying the scar → invariant → machine-readable gene → proof obligation → evidence-bearing finding → staged-promotion chain. So Genome/CSC absolutely influenced the candidate population and the reasoning that produced E-00.

But when I inspected the actual RS-001 authority ledger just now, it pins:

* HSP/OARR v0.3
* Loop+ V2
* Microseed P₀
* Rahl/CIC
* supplied HSP 270–274 state

**There is no dedicated current Genome/CSC authority artifact in that ledger.**

And E-00 is currently qualified by its own tests/OARR machinery, **not by a Microseed-local CSC project profile, Genome obligations, or CSC evidence provider.**

So the truthful answer is:

> **Genome/CSC influenced the project. CSC has not yet been structurally integrated into the Microseed machine.**

That is a gap I want to correct **before E-01**.

And there is substantially more current CSC machinery available than the light treatment in RS-001 implies. Your current CIC evidence shows an actual CSC seam with `engineering/lineage/csc/**`, `tools/assurance/csc/**`, project profiles, native gates, lineage-presence checks, doctrine-surface checks, discrimination cases, audit-only/veto authority semantics, and an explicit rule that CSC may inspect CIC while CIC runtime must not import CSC. 

The recovered reality-compiler evidence also shows CSC already operating as something much closer to the direction we want for Microseed: target-aware project profiles, rule discrimination, lineage anchoring, doctrine coverage, runtime judgment distinctions, and evidence-bearing gate outputs. 

And CIC's actual promotion history shows this isn't just dormant doctrine: a real target pass ran semantic tests, CSC self-audit, discrimination, runtime-package exclusion, full authored-source tests, real-project audit, and then kept **CSC enforcement/veto authority explicitly at `NONE`** while the target itself retained promotion authority. 

That last part is especially useful.

## CSC should not become “Microseed's boss”

The appropriate architecture is:

```text
                         MICROSEED
                             │
            ┌────────────────┴────────────────┐
            │                                 │
         HSP/OARR                         GENOME / CSC
    research methodology                assurance substrate
            │                                 │
    expand / cull / PDVER               genes / obligations
            │                            detectors/providers
            │                            evidence findings
            │                                 │
            └──────────────┬──────────────────┘
                           ↓
                    EMBODIMENT ARM
                           ↓
                        E-00/E-01...
                           ↓
                      REAL TARGET
```

But authority stays separated:

```text
Genome
    = what guarantee/invariant are we trying to preserve?

CSC
    = what qualified mechanism can determine whether
      this embodiment satisfies/violates/is-unknown
      with respect to that guarantee?

HSP/OARR
    = what does the total evidence mean and
      what gets researched/attacked next?

Target / promotion process
    = what actually earns embodiment authority?
```

I **do not** want:

```text
CSC says green
→ Microseed architecture canonical
```

That would violate the lessons CSC itself has already learned.

---

## And Microseed is an unusually good CSC laboratory

This may actually improve both projects recursively.

Take the four scars from E-00:

### Scar 1

**ambient CWD was an undeclared input**

Genome candidate:

```text
GENE:
DECLARED_EXECUTION_INPUT_CLOSURE
```

Obligation:

> consequential execution inputs must be declared or explicitly classified outside the assurance boundary.

CSC finding could then be:

```text
OBLIGATION
execution dependency set ⊆ declared dependency surface

EVIDENCE
contestant accessed /foo/bar outside declared execution frame

DISPOSITION
VIOLATED
```

### Scar 2

**declared artifact ≠ actually executed artifact**

Genome candidate:

```text
GENE:
EXECUTED_ARTIFACT_CORRESPONDENCE
```

That's almost directly descended from CIC's candidate/target scars.

### Scar 3

**one run ≠ deterministic replay**

Genome candidate:

```text
GENE:
REPLAY_CLAIM_REQUIRES_RECURRENCE
```

Although we'd need to pressure whether that's truly a reusable gene or merely E-00 benchmark semantics.

### Scar 4

**coarse RSS/time meter ≠ fine performance authority**

That could generate something more general like:

```text
MEASUREMENT_RESOLUTION_BOUNDS_CLAIM
```

where a metric cannot adjudicate differences smaller than its qualified resolution/variance envelope.

Again: **candidate**, not canon.

This is exactly what Genome is for.

Instead of letting E-00 scars remain prose in a report, we can ask whether they can become:

```text
SCAR
 ↓
candidate invariant
 ↓
Genome IR
 ↓
proof obligation
 ↓
CSC provider
 ↓
hostile specimens
 ↓
finding
```

Microseed becomes another real scar quarry for the coding-standard project.

And Genome/CSC becomes an independent assurance pressure on Microseed.

**Recursive feed in both directions.**

---

## It also helps with the biggest danger in E-01

We're about to test:

> typed consequential boundary + current-qualified-capability routing

against a boring baseline.

Without Genome/CSC, it is easy for us to end up with:

```text
candidate seems cleaner
candidate passed tasks
candidate handled churn
→ candidate wins
```

CSC gives us another dimension:

> **What guarantee did each architecture actually make machine-observable?**

Two systems can score identically while one produces far better evidence for:

* wrong capability selection
* stale qualification
* undeclared action
* authority violation
* malformed argument
* common-mode dependency
* false UNKNOWN/false green.

That matters because the whole Microseed thesis isn't merely:

> do more tasks with fewer bytes.

It's closer to:

> **get disproportionate effective capability while retaining truthful, inspectable control under severe resource constraints.**

So assurance-efficiency itself should eventually become part of the frontier:

```text
capability
resource cost
robustness
auditability
false-green rate
truthful-UNKNOWN rate
assurance cost
```

not merely accuracy/RAM.

# One important caution

I searched your available artifact library for the current CSC lineage rather than assuming the old Genome/CSC conception was still authoritative.

There are multiple generations.

Some older Forge/Genome work contains much more speculative things like universal PPGs, thermodynamic annotations, broad soundness claims, etc. I would **not import that wholesale**.

The current CIC-integrated CSC evidence is more mature and restrained: project-specific profiles, target-aware gates, explicit doctrine coverage, discrimination fixtures, lineage anchors, audit-only authority, and explicit coverage ceilings. 

That's the substrate I want to quarry.

Same rule as everywhere else:

> **Import surviving guarantees and scars, not historical machinery merely because it has “CSC” in the filename.**

---

So: **yes, you caught a real omission before it became expensive.**

We did not forget Genome/CSC conceptually.

We **did fail to make current Genome/CSC a peer authority and active assurance participant in Microseed RS-001.**

I would treat this as a pre-E-01 correction rather than retroactively pretending RS-001 already did it.

The clean next move is a bounded **`MICROSEED-CSC-INT-001`** integration/reconciliation slice:

```text

        ↓

        ↓

        ↓

```

And **if CSC does not buy independent discrimination/evidence quality over the existing E-00/OARR checks, we do not keep a ceremonial CSC layer just because it's ours.**

That is the standard it should have to survive too.
Done. **CSC/Genome is now structurally inside Microseed**, rather than merely influencing it through ancestry.

CSC is **`audit_only`**. Its veto, enforcement, promotion, and production authority are all **`NONE`**.

That is directly consistent with the more mature CIC CSC seam I recovered: its current project profile/gate/report machinery separates findings, gate results, audit results and rule-discrimination cases; its discrimination surface explicitly includes known-good, known-bad, near-miss and false-positive classes.  The existing CIC embodiment likewise keeps CSC outside the runtime payload and treats `CSC → project inspection` as lawful while `runtime → CSC import` is forbidden.  Actual CIC qualification evidence shows that pattern operating as `audit_only`, with both veto and enforcement authority remaining `NONE` even when CSC is clean. 

One authority ceiling matters: **the actual current CSC source archive was not mounted here.** What I built is therefore a Microseed-local **adaptation from the current CSC forensic/recon evidence**, not a byte-preserving CSC source import. The package says so explicitly.

## E-01 verdict: NARROWED

The three-way arena was:

```text
B0
simple JSON/dict
+
health/current bit
+
scope/action checks

B1
boring JSON/dict
+
explicit CURRENT / UNAVAILABLE / UNKNOWN
+
currentness age
+
authority scope
+
identity conflict
+
evidence propagation

C1
same semantics as B1
+
explicit dataclasses/enums/typed boundary
```

Across **15 hostile/static routing situations**, with every case receiving two-run E-00 replay:

```text
                         DECISION     EVIDENCE     REPLAY
B0 health-bit JSON        10/15        11/15        15/15
B1 semantic JSON          15/15        15/15        15/15
C1 typed boundary         15/15        15/15        15/15
```

B0's five meaningful losses were exactly the sort of semantic-collapse failures we're interested in:

```text
all UNKNOWN
UNKNOWN → unavailable
instead of DEFER_UNKNOWN

stale-only capability
stale → ACT
instead of DEFER_UNKNOWN

stale primary + current backup
stale primary → ACT
instead of current backup

scope-denied + authorized UNKNOWN fallback
→ DENY_AUTHORITY
instead of DEFER_UNKNOWN

conflicting records for same capability identity
last-write collapse
instead of UNKNOWN_CONFLICT
```

So we have evidence that **the distinctions matter**.

But then the anti-flattery result bites.

B1 and C1 were functionally identical in this arena.

And the explicit typed implementation cost more source machinery:

```text
B1 semantic JSON
2,807 bytes
57 logical lines

C1 typed boundary
3,991 bytes
100 logical lines
```

That's roughly **42% more source bytes and 75% more logical lines** for no observed decision/evidence advantage in E-01.

I am **not** using their RSS figures to make a runtime-efficiency claim because E-00's process RSS/time telemetry is still too coarse for fine contestant ordering.

Therefore:

> **The semantics survived. The bespoke typed representation did not.**

More exactly:

```text
C-01
QUALIFIED_CAPABILITY_CURRENTNESS_AND_ROUTING

→ PRESSURE-SUPPORTED
   within static E-01 laboratory only
```

while:

```text
C-03
TYPED_ACTION_EVIDENCE_BOUNDARY

→ NARROWED

MACHINE_CHECKABLE_ACTION_EVIDENCE_BOUNDARY
representation remains open
```

So **no bespoke Cognitive IR gets architecture authority from this**.

A strict JSON/schema implementation is currently just as entitled to embody the guarantee.

That is exactly the kind of cull we wanted.

is now embodied in the experiment rather than merely being philosophy.

# Genome/CSC absorbed the scars

The Genome registry now has **six** candidates.

The four E-00 candidates remain, and E-01 added:

### `MS-GC-005`

**CONSEQUENTIAL_ACTION_REQUIRES_CURRENT_QUALIFIED_CAPABILITY**

Candidate invariant:

> A consequential action may route only through a capability whose relevant action/scope authority exists and whose qualified currentness remains inside its declared currentness bound. Relevant UNKNOWN cannot be laundered into either current or unavailable.

This came directly from the stale/UNKNOWN B0 failures.

### `MS-GC-006`

**CAPABILITY_IDENTITY_CONFLICT_MUST_NOT_LAST_WRITE**

Candidate invariant:

> Conflicting simultaneous records for a consequential capability identity require explicit conflict/UNKNOWN unless some qualified reconciliation mechanism establishes which record has authority.

The important near-miss is encoded too:

```text
same identity
+
exact same record twice

≠ necessarily conflict
```

because duplicate evidence and contradictory evidence are different things.

Neither rule is promoted.

```text
enforcement_eligible = false
```

CSC remains:

```text
audit_only
veto             NONE
enforcement      NONE
promotion        NONE
```

The updated CSC discrimination surface reports **all six rules discriminated**.

And its E-01 providers are explicitly labeled:

```text
MICROSEED-E01-001_REUSED_ARENA_LINEAGE
```

so CSC does **not** get counted as an independent vote for the arena that generated its evidence.

`independent_proof_claimed = false`

still holds.

# And E-01 exposed its own boundary

Everything we just established is **static snapshot semantics**.

We have not established what happens across:

```text
CURRENT
   ↓
observer gets stale
   ↓
capability actually fails
   ↓
revocation arrives
   ↓
alternate route becomes current
   ↓
primary recovers
   ↓
requalification occurs
```

Those transitions are where this mechanism can still be full of shit.

For example:

```text
capability was current at t0
request selected it
revocation occurred at t1
action executed at t2
```

What exactly has authority?

Or:

```text
old observation: CURRENT
new observation: UNKNOWN
recovery signal: AVAILABLE
qualification not rerun
```

Can routing resume?

It shouldn't merely because something looks alive again.

That's why the breadth sweep **did not blindly select E-02 memory next**.

The most load-bearing continuation is now:

> **`ATTN-E04-CAPABILITY-CHURN`**

because E-04 directly attacks the thing that survived E-01.

E-02 remains preserved and deferred.

## E-04 verdict

The three contestants produced a clean separation:

```text
B0 — LAST-WRITE + LOCAL RECHECK
decision:  7/15
evidence: 12/15

B1 — VERSIONED REVALIDATION
decision: 13/15
evidence: 15/15

C1 — VERSIONED REVALIDATION + TARGET FENCE
decision: 15/15
evidence: 15/15
```

The important part is **where B1 still fails**.

It handles:

* revocation;
* stale/out-of-order observations;
* recovery without requalification;
* explicit requalification;
* generation rollback;
* same-generation conflict;
* expiry before local execution;
* unrelated capability churn.

But it loses exactly two cases:

```text
PREPARE under generation N
        ↓
authority revoked before external EFFECT
        ↓
detached command still executes

and

PREPARE before lease expiry
        ↓
lease expires
        ↓
detached external EFFECT occurs afterward
```

C1 closes **exactly those two** by requiring the consequence boundary itself to enforce the expected authority generation and validity interval.

So the surviving law is narrower than “use fencing”:

> **Local revalidation can be insufficient once a consequential command becomes detached from the authority state that justified it. Where such detachment exists, the consequence boundary needs an equivalent current-authority check—such as a generation fence, expiry check, or another mechanism that proves the command is still entitled to take effect.**

And equally importantly:

> **E-04 did not establish general fencing dominance.**

For synchronous/local effects, C1 bought no observed decision advantage over boring B1.

That keeps us out of the Cathedral.

Primary-source pressure is consistent with the mechanism without becoming project authority: Chubby describes carrying a monotonically advancing lock-acquisition value to the receiving server so delayed operations with older values can be rejected; Kubernetes and Vault similarly expose explicit lease/currentness and renewal/revocation semantics. ([Google Cloud Storage][1])

## Hostile pressure

The E-04 hostile laboratory rejected **10/10 mutants**, including:

```text
recovery signal → silently restores authority       REJECTED
older generation → rolls authority backward         REJECTED
same-generation conflict → last-write wins          REJECTED
prepared generation mismatch → ignored               REJECTED
expiry → ignored                                     REJECTED
conflict → evidence dropped                          REJECTED

target fence → disabled                              REJECTED
effect-time expiry → disabled                        REJECTED
unrelated/global fence → false positive              REJECTED
correct effect → evidence silently dropped           REJECTED
```

The unrelated-fence mutant matters because it prevents:

> “fencing worked”

from mutating into:

> “one giant global epoch solves everything.”

Identity/scope still matter.

---

## Genome/CSC moved with the scars

There are now **9 Genome candidates, 0 promoted**.

E-04 added:

```text
MS-GC-007
AUTHORITY_GENERATION_MUST_NOT_ROLL_BACK
```

An observation with less authority recency cannot silently supersede a qualified newer authority state.

```text
MS-GC-008
RECOVERY_SIGNAL_DOES_NOT_RESTORE_AUTHORITY
```

“Looks alive again” is not requalification.

```text
MS-GC-009
DETACHED_EFFECT_REQUIRES_CONSEQUENCE_BOUNDARY_AUTHORITY
```

When execution becomes detached from the authority state used to prepare it, consequential effect requires a qualified authority-currentness mechanism at the effect boundary.

`MS-GC-005` was also sharpened: current-qualified capability routing now survives **local temporal churn**, but it explicitly does **not** claim to solve the detached-effect boundary.

CSC remains:

```text
mode          audit_only
veto          NONE
enforcement   NONE
promotion     NONE
```

Its current self-audit reports:

```text
final_clean                 true
all 9 rules discriminated   true
independent proof claimed   false
```

And the current CSC hostile battery rejected **11/11 mutants**.

The E-04 CSC providers explicitly reuse E-04 arena lineage, so CSC does **not** become a second epistemic vote merely by re-expressing the same evidence.

# What actually survived

At this point Microseed has earned a little more anatomy:

```text
CURRENT / UNKNOWN / AUTHORITY
            +
MONOTONIC AUTHORITY GENERATION
            +
EXPLICIT REQUALIFICATION
            +
IDENTITY-CONFLICT PRESERVATION
            +
EVIDENCE-BEARING CONSEQUENCE
```

are increasingly well supported in our bounded synthetic laboratories.

And conditionally:

```text
DETACHED CONSEQUENTIAL EFFECT
            ↓
CONSEQUENCE-BOUNDARY
CURRENT-AUTHORITY CHECK
```

survives too.

But we have **not** earned:

```text
universal fencing framework
distributed lease subsystem
bespoke Cognitive IR
general cognitive architecture
fine performance superiority
target honesty
network/event-delivery closure
```

Architecture authority remains **NONE**.

## Breadth sweep

I am **not** taking another capability-boundary slice next.

E-01 and E-04 were two consecutive turns through essentially the same semantic family. The breadth sweep therefore selects:

> **`ATTN-E02-TEMPORAL-MEMORY`**

The next fight should pressure the old **hot-core / cold-world / lineage-bearing external memory** idea against genuinely boring machinery:

```text
SQLite
latest-value table
append-only event log
explicit supersession
```

before we invent a custom memory architecture.

`ATTN-E03-CONSEQUENCE-BOUNDARY-AUTHORITY` remains open for later because E-04 only solved **whether a stale command may take effect**. It did not establish:

> effect observed
> ≠ effect caused by us
> ≠ intended consequence achieved.

**Hard stop is intact. E-02 has not started.**

[1]: https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-research2023-media/pubtools/4444.pdf "https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-research2023-media/pubtools/4444.pdf"



**E-03 is complete.** The result is strong, narrow, and useful: we earned consequence/causal semantics without earning another custom architecture layer.

[**Download Microseed Mainline — E03-001 Consequence-Boundary Authority**](sandbox:/mnt/data/MICROSEED_MAINLINE_E03_001_2026-08-17.zip)

**SHA-256:** `5d077535b5234fdd1c11578c9d5980233adf75102709910ef3b85cfc88b95595`

## E-03 verdict: NARROWED

The arena deliberately separated three progressively stronger mechanisms:

```text
B0
request acceptance
+
target-state observation

B1
B0
+
command/correlation identity

C1
request acceptance
+
target-bound effect evidence
+
command/effect correspondence
+
post-effect observation
+
separate goal evaluation
```

The result from the sealed archive:

```text
                         DECISION    EVIDENCE    REPLAY
B0 ACK + observation       5/18        7/18       18/18

B1 correlated observation 10/18        7/18       18/18

C1 effect boundary
   + goal check            18/18       18/18      18/18
```

So several distinctions have now earned real synthetic pressure support:

```text
COMMAND ACCEPTED
        ≠
EFFECT OCCURRED
        ≠
EFFECT ATTRIBUTABLE TO OUR COMMAND
        ≠
GOAL OBSERVED
        ≠
OUR COMMAND CAUSED THE GOAL
        ≠
INTENDED GOAL SATISFIED
```

This is not philosophical nitpicking. The arena contains concrete worlds where every collapse above produces the wrong answer.

A request can be accepted and never act.

The desired state can appear because **someone else** caused it.

An observation can carry our command ID and still lack evidence that our command caused the effect.

Our command can genuinely cause an effect and nevertheless fail its goal.

The goal can already be true, making our operation a `NOOP`: goal satisfied, **zero causal authorship**.

And an effect receipt can be legitimate while the post-effect observation shows the state was subsequently reversed.

The external pressure agrees with the shape of those distinctions. HTTP's `202 Accepted` explicitly means processing has not completed and might never ultimately be acted on, so protocol acceptance is demonstrably not generic effect authority. ([RFC Editor][1]) W3C Trace Context standardizes identifiers that let distributed operations be correlated, while OpenTelemetry uses propagated contexts, parent-child relationships, links and events to represent related operations. That supports correlation/lineage as useful machinery, but neither specification turns a correlation identifier into independent proof that some real-world semantic goal was achieved. ([W3C][2])

That last conclusion is deliberately an inference from what those mechanisms establish, not something I'm attributing to the specifications themselves.

## The anti-Cathedral result is just as important

C1 is **not** some elaborate Microseed causal engine.

It's boring Python/JSON semantics.

So the thing that survived is:

```text
CONSEQUENCE-EVIDENCE CONTRACT
+
CAUSAL CORRESPONDENCE
+
SEPARATE GOAL ASSESSMENT
```

not:

```text
Custom Agency IR™
Causal Ontology Engine™
Intent Execution Graph™
```

There is still **zero cognitive-architecture promotion**.

That's becoming a really encouraging pattern in this project: ancestral ideas are surviving increasingly often as **small semantic obligations implemented by boring substrates**, rather than as the large machinery their original names might have suggested.

## Ranger #3 went directly after the 18/18

C1 was attacked with ten mutations. **10/10 were rejected.**

They specifically tried to make the system:

* treat acceptance as success;
* treat correlation without effect evidence as causality;
* borrow a receipt from the wrong target;
* ignore effect identity;
* use a pre-effect observation as consequence evidence;
* treat `APPLIED` as proof the goal succeeded without observing the goal;
* claim authorship over an independently achieved goal;
* ignore contradictory effect receipts;
* count a `NOOP` as causal success;
* silently drop consequence evidence.

That gives us a much stronger statement than merely “C1 got all the test cases.”

The discriminating surfaces themselves are carrying load.

## Genome/CSC absorbed two more scars

There are now **13 Genome candidates and 0 promoted standards**.

`MS-GC-012 — CONSEQUENCE_CLAIM_REQUIRES_RELEVANT_EFFECT_EVIDENCE`

The candidate law is roughly:

> Request acceptance or correlation may establish request lineage, but a consequential effect claim needs evidence from the relevant qualified consequence boundary or some equivalent mechanism.

And:

`MS-GC-013 — CAUSAL_AUTHORSHIP_AND_GOAL_SATISFACTION_MUST_NOT_COLLAPSE`

which captures:

> “our action caused an effect” and “the intended goal is satisfied” are separate proof obligations.

That second one came from both directions:

```text
GOAL MET
+
OUR ACTION DID NOT CAUSE IT
```

and:

```text
OUR ACTION CAUSED AN EFFECT
+
GOAL NOT MET
```

The old P₀ `CAUSAL_AUTHORSHIP_SEPARATION` candidate therefore no longer sits around merely as an attractive benchmark idea. It has been reclassified into a pressure-supported project semantic law within the E-03 bound.

`core_relation_growth = 0`.

We did not invent the distinction during the experiment. Reality started qualifying one we already had.

CSC remains exactly where it belongs:

```text
authority mode       audit_only
veto authority       NONE
enforcement          NONE
promotion            NONE

Genome candidates    13
all discriminated    true
independent proof    false
```

The new E-03 providers explicitly carry:

`MICROSEED-E03-001_REUSED_ARENA_LINEAGE`

so CSC does not magically become an independent causal witness by translating arena evidence into Genome findings.

Its hostile battery is now **15/15 rejected**, including new attacks that try to launder command correlation into causality and `NOOP` goal satisfaction into causal authorship.

## We also improved the assurance machinery rather than merely paying its tax

CSC initially inherited every previous project command gate and was re-running the entirety of E-00/E-01/E-04/E-02 while evaluating E-03.

That stopped changing the evidence state and started producing timeout noise.

So I changed the shape.

E-03 CSC now uses:

```text
SEALED E-02 PARENT
        ↓
exact hashes for unchanged
E-00 / E-01 / E-02 / E-04 embodiment members
        +
CURRENT E-03 GATES
        +
CURRENT 13-RULE CSC DISCRIMINATION
```

Any modification to a prior embodiment member reopens qualification.

Unchanged bytes don't get ceremonially re-executed merely because another slice happened.

That's essentially **incremental assurance**.

The CSC hostile runner received the same treatment. A mutant attacking `MS-GC-012` now executes that rule's provider rather than pointlessly reacquiring all thirteen rules.

The CSC hostile runtime dropped to about **23 seconds** while preserving the proposition being attacked.

That is exactly what the methodology ought to do to itself.

# Where Microseed stands now

We have still promoted **no cognitive architecture**.

But a surprisingly coherent little semantic skeleton is starting to accumulate:

```text
TEMPORAL MEMORY
valid time ≠ time-known
current projection ≠ lineage authority

        ↓

CAPABILITY AUTHORITY
identity ≠ current qualification
recovery ≠ restored authority
older generation ≠ current authority

        ↓

ACTION AUTHORITY
prepare authority ≠ effect-time authority

        ↓

CONSEQUENCE
command accepted ≠ effect occurred
correlation ≠ causal authorship
effect occurred ≠ goal achieved
goal achieved ≠ we caused it
```

And importantly, most of that is currently embodied using **SQLite, dictionaries, JSON, integer generations, explicit IDs and small deterministic rules**.

That's fucking excellent for the Ant-Man thesis.

We're finding out how much semantic discipline can be purchased **before** paying for intelligence.

## Breadth sweep

I did **not** select another action/consequence refinement.

The next frontier is:

> **`ATTN-E05-ACTIVE-DISCRIMINATION`**

We've now become fairly good at saying:

```text
UNKNOWN
```

truthfully.

The next genuinely cognitive question is:

> **Given several possible observations/tools and a decision-relevant UNKNOWN, can a tiny system determine which observation is actually worth acquiring?**

That moves us from:

```text
preserve uncertainty
```

to:

```text
actively destroy the right uncertainty
```

under resource cost.

And it gives us another obvious boring opponent:

```text
fixed observation order
/
query everything
/
cheapest-first
```

against something that reasons about **expected discrimination value**.

That is the first frontier so far where I expect we may begin touching something recognizable as a **cognitive mechanism**, rather than infrastructure that keeps cognition from lying to itself.

**Hard stop honored. E-05 has not started.**

[1]: https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9110.html "https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9110.html"
[2]: https://www.w3.org/TR/trace-context/ "https://www.w3.org/TR/trace-context/"

The cross-project mine **materially changed the next slice**, and E-05 is now complete.

[**Download Microseed Mainline — E05-001 Active Discrimination**](sandbox:/mnt/data/MICROSEED_MAINLINE_E05_001_2026-08-17.zip)

**SHA-256:** `8c2a0a81b33ed14e04edd2a833b09ffd06b329b7d2637928c8d57f55f34e22ee`

## What the mine contributed

The most useful recurrence was stronger than the original E-05 wording.

CIC had already separated **action for effect** from **action for information**, with bounded escalation from passive observation through targeted query and reversible perturbation—and explicitly subordinated information gain to authorization, safety, proportionality, and reversibility. 

FtD independently had essentially the physical version: observation quality is a resource, and the organism can spend movement/geometry/sensor attention specifically to reduce uncertainty rather than merely pursue an effect. 

Aedifex contributed bounded exploratory calibration against environmental consequence rather than privileged internal truth. 

And the older KarnOS/Pattern-Farm lineage contained cost-aware dispatch and later adaptive/bandit routing ideas—but that was useful mainly as a warning **not to jump to a learned router before a deterministic mechanism loses**. 

That compressed E-05 to:

> **Do not maximize information in the abstract. Spend scarce acquisition budget on eliminating uncertainty that can actually change the required decision.**

That turned out to matter.

## E-05 result

Five contestants:

```text
B0  fixed observation order
B1  cheapest first
B2  generic information-gain greedy
C1  decision-relevant information greedy
C2  bounded minimax decision discrimination
```

Across 17 cases:

```text
                         CLOSED     QUERIES    COST
B0 fixed order            14/17       17       31
B1 cheapest first         12/17       18       27
B2 generic info greedy    13/17       17       26
C1 decision greedy        14/17       16       25
C2 bounded minimax        17/17       15       29
```

This separates three things we had previously lumped together:

```text
HYPOTHESIS INFORMATION
        ≠
DECISION-RELEVANT INFORMATION
        ≠
LOOKAHEAD OVER FUTURE
DISCRIMINATION + COST
```

C1 beats generic information selection because it doesn't care about distinguishing hypotheses that imply the **same decision**.

But C1 still loses three worlds.

Two are proper lookahead traps. The locally best next query leaves a later ambiguity it cannot afford to close.

The third is nastier: a cheap, genuinely useful observation consumes enough budget that the decisive later observation becomes unaffordable.

C2 anticipates those branches and closes all 17.

That is probably the first thing Microseed has earned that I would call recognizably **cognitive machinery**, rather than infrastructure preventing cognition from lying.

But that sentence is tightly bounded.

## C2 is surprisingly small

The actual selection strategy is only about:

```text
1,430 source bytes
27 logical strategy lines
```

It doesn't contain:

* an LLM;
* learned policy;
* neural inference;
* TQ2;
* probability estimates;
* bespoke Cognitive IR;
* bandit learner.

It's essentially exact finite search over:

```text
surviving hypotheses
×
lawful observations
×
possible outcomes
×
remaining acquisition budget
```

with the objective being **worst-case decision closure**, not world-state identification.

That's very Ant-Man.

But its combinatorial scaling is unresolved. I am **not** promoting “bounded minimax” into Microseed's universal planner.

---

# Anti-flattery caught a real false green

The first implementation gave C2 a beautiful result.

Then we attacked the measurement boundary.

The arena had two flaws.

First, contestants received a case object containing the hidden hypothesis. The intended policies weren't using it, but the boundary made cheating possible.

Second—and worse—the evaluator trusted:

```text
contestant.reported_total_cost
```

So a mutant could perform expensive observations and simply report:

```text
cost = 0
```

The first hostile run therefore rejected only:

```text
9 / 12
```

mutants.

That's a genuine experimental false-green route.

I changed the authority boundary.

Now:

```text
POLICY
receives only observable hypothesis/model state
        ↓
returns requested observation

ARENA
owns hidden reality
        ↓
realizes observation
        ↓
assigns authoritative evidence ID
        ↓
recomputes actual acquisition cost
        ↓
updates surviving hypotheses
```

The policy is no longer authoritative about either hidden state **or its own cost**.

After that correction:

**12/12 hostile mutants were rejected.**

Those attacks include unsafe/disproportionate/irreversible/unauthorized information actions, cost falsification, evidence dropping, hidden-state exposure, removing lookahead, swapping minimax for best-case reasoning, and laundering generic information gain into decision discrimination.

That false start paid rent.

---

# One subtle result matters a lot

Microseed does **not** need to identify the true world completely before acting.

Suppose:

```text
H1 → DEFER
H2 → DEFER
```

If our observations reduce the state to `{H1,H2}`, then the relevant decision is already resolved even though reality remains ambiguous.

Therefore:

```text
WORLD IDENTIFICATION
≠
DECISION CLOSURE
```

This is an important capability-per-resource optimization.

A tiny machine shouldn't spend its remaining budget resolving distinctions that **cannot change what it should do**.

That may become one of Microseed's deeper compression advantages.

---

# Genome/CSC results

Genome now has:

```text
15 candidate rules
0 promoted standards
```

E-05 added:

**`MS-GC-014 — DISCRIMINATION_OBJECTIVE_MUST_MATCH_DECISION_OBLIGATION`**

A mechanism intended to resolve a decision cannot substitute generic hypothesis discrimination when the additional distinctions do not affect the decision obligation.

And:

**`MS-GC-015 — INFORMATION_ACTION_REMAINS_GOVERNED_ACTION`**

An action taken for information remains subject to authorization, safety, proportionality, reversibility where relevant, and evidence requirements.

That second rule is important because otherwise:

> “but I was just gathering information”

becomes an authority escape hatch.

CSC remains:

```text
BOUNDED_DECISION_RELEVANT_ACTIVE_DISCRIMINATION
```

with scope approximately:

> Given a finite explicit hypothesis set, modeled observation partitions, lawful observation/action constraints and bounded integer acquisition cost, choose observations that reduce decision-relevant ambiguity; bounded lookahead may dominate greedy choice where future query affordability or branch structure matters.

It does **not** establish:

Not before.
