# What the cross-project mine says about Microseed so far

An increasingly coherent pattern is emerging across the experiments:

```text
E-01
don't collapse epistemic state

E-04
don't collapse authority across time

E-02
don't collapse truth-time and knowledge-time

E-03
don't collapse action, effect, authorship and goal

E-05
don't collapse information with useful discrimination
```

And the striking thing is that we keep buying these capabilities with **small deterministic semantic mechanisms**, not large intelligence machinery.

That changes how I view the thesis slightly.

Microseed may gain a surprising amount of apparent intelligence not by making the tiny learned core vastly smarter, but by making the surrounding machine **extremely difficult to confuse about what problem it is actually solving**.

That's becoming an experimentally interesting hypothesis in its own right.

## Breadth-selected next frontier

I am **not** continuing deeper into active discrimination.

Selected next:

> **`ATTN-E06-NOVEL-COMPOSITION`**

Because this is the other central Microseed promise:

```text
small individually limited mechanisms
        ↓
qualified composition
        ↓
capability not explicitly hand-authored
as one monolithic behavior
```

And the mine gives E-06 a much stronger framing than our original P₀ wording.

FtD suggests capability composition and semantic compression upward. Aedifex warns us that formed phenotype must be distinguished from governing rules. Forge/Pattern Genome gives us compatibility/conflict machinery. And CIC gives us the crucial warning that **available parts do not automatically imply an authorized higher-order capability**.

So E-06 should ask:

> **Can a tiny deterministic system compose already-qualified primitives into a correct novel capability under previously unseen task combinations, while refusing compositions whose invariants, authority, resource requirements, or effect contracts conflict—and does that beat a boring table of predeclared workflows?**

That's the right next dragon.

**E-06 has not started.**
Yes. **It helps materially, and it sharpens E-06 before we start it.**

The important correction is that the surviving structure is not “some symbols we vaguely used.” The document preserves a very specific epistemic firewall:

> **REFERENT != OBSERVATION != RECORD != MODEL != WARRANTED CLAIM != OPERATIONAL ACTION** 

and the accompanying diagnostic vocabulary:

```text
Q = explicit query / frame contract
A = acquisition / intervention regime
R = records / evidence corpus
M = competing model / hypothesis family
Γ = typed guarantee family
W = query-scoped warrant
```

with the document explicitly using the form:

```text
W(m, q | F, A, R)
```

for the warrant of model `m` with respect to query `q`, conditioned on the declared frame, acquisition regime, and records. 

And your correction about the stars/daggers is right: **this artifact does not substantiate literal suffix notation such as `REFERENT*` or `RECORD†`.** I should not reconstruct meanings for symbols we have not actually recovered.

One qualification matters: the DOCX itself is explicitly a **closed, non-canonical evaluation artifact**. So I would treat the firewall above as the **surviving formulation preserved by this experiment**, not silently promote the entire OIG/EDG ledger to current project authority. The document itself warns that even its hard-law ledger remains non-canonical and subject to later breakpoint audit. 

### Why this matters right now

It gives us a better way to frame **E-06 Novel Composition**.

Before this recovery, E-06 was headed toward:

```text
qualified primitives
        ↓
compatibility / resource / authority checking
        ↓
novel composed behavior
```

That's still right, but incomplete.

The recovered firewall tells us that a composition engine can fail even when every primitive is individually valid if it silently collapses stages of the epistemic/action chain.

For example:

```text
record says sensor X reported OPEN
        ↓
model predicts route A is clear
        ↓
claim "route A is clear"
        ↓
action GO
```

Those are **four different authorities**.

A composer must not turn:

```text
RECORD
→ OPERATIONAL ACTION
```

merely because the types line up.

The missing intermediate question is:

```text
What query are we answering?
What acquisition regime produced the evidence?
What model transformed it?
Which guarantee is required?
What warrant exists for this claim under that query?
What consequence scope is authorized?
```

That gives E-06 a much nastier and much more useful adversary.

## I would therefore change the E-06 contestants

Not by building an OIG/EDG framework into Microseed.

Use the recovered relations primarily as **evaluation semantics**.

```text
B0 — PREDECLARED WORKFLOW TABLE
Only solves explicitly authored task combinations.

B1 — STRUCTURAL COMPOSER
Can connect primitives whenever input/output/resource
and basic authority contracts appear compatible.

C1 — GUARANTEE/WARRANT-AWARE COMPOSER
B1 +
preserves the distinctions needed to establish
whether the resulting composition is actually entitled
to make the required claim/action.
```

That gives us two independent questions:

```text
Does composition buy novel capability?
```

and:

```text
Does guarantee/warrant-aware composition prevent
semantically invalid novelty that structural compatibility admits?
```

That's a much better experiment.

### The document also gives us specific hostile cases

Several of its surviving relations map directly into E-06 pressure.

**AB-01 — Guarantee-Sufficient Abstraction** says a composed or higher-level representation may discard detail only if it preserves or makes recoverable the distinctions necessary for the guarantee/query/intervention it claims to support. 

So an E-06 mutant can compose two valid primitives through a lossy intermediate representation that deletes something needed downstream.

Structurally valid.

Semantically invalid.

**CS-01 — Consequence-Scope Alignment** says available causal capability does not automatically imply justified consequence authority. 

So:

```text
primitive A can read
primitive B can write
A→B technically composes
```

does **not** establish:

```text
the resulting composed capability
is authorized to produce B's consequence
```

**GC-01 — Guarantee-Support Concordance** gives us another composition failure:

```text
P1 guarantees Γ1
P2 guarantees Γ2

P1 + P2
→ composer claims stronger Γ3
```

without showing that the composition actually supports Γ3. The ledger explicitly rejects this sort of unsupported guarantee exposure. 

**WT-01 — Warrant Traceability** means a novel composed claim may need enough lineage retained for later challenge, expiry, recomputation, or audit; “we composed it successfully” is not lineage. 

And the document's `DL-03` distinction is almost tailor-made for hostile composition:

```text
possible coupling
!= actual coupling
!= observed coupling
!= inferred coupling
!= relied-upon coupling
!= authorized coupling
```



That could catch some nasty cases where a composer confuses:

> “these two modules can communicate”

with:

> “this path exists, occurred, was observed, is reliable enough to depend upon, and is authorized.”

Those are absolutely not the same proposition.

---

## It also retrospectively unifies what Microseed has been finding

Without forcing this document onto the project, the recurrence is striking.

```text
E-02
referent time != recorded/known time
snapshot != history

E-03
command != effect
observation != causal authorship
effect != goal

E-05
available information != warranted decision relevance

OIG/EDG
referent != observation != record != model
!= warranted claim != operational action
```

The experimental Microseed slices have independently been rediscovering **local instances of the firewall**.

That does **not** mean the whole firewall is now proven by Microseed.

But it makes it a very high-value pressure lens.

And one relation from the ledger may be especially central to the emerging Microseed thesis:

> **A higher-level abstraction earns authority only for the queries and guarantees whose relevant distinctions survive the abstraction.** 

Because Microseed is fundamentally an exercise in brutal compression.

The danger isn't merely losing information.

It's:

> **losing a distinction and continuing to behave as though the higher-level representation retained the authority that depended upon that distinction.**

That's much more precise.

So yes—**this definitely helps**.

I would carry forward:

```text
REFERENT
  ≠ OBSERVATION
  ≠ RECORD
  ≠ MODEL
  ≠ WARRANTED CLAIM
  ≠ OPERATIONAL ACTION
```

and:

```text
Q / A / R / M / Γ / W
```

as an **evaluation lens for E-06**, not as a mandatory Microseed runtime ontology.

If C1 needs only three of those distinctions to beat B1, we keep three.

If boring composition can preserve the guarantees without explicitly materializing `Q/A/R/M/Γ/W`, the notation stays outside the organism.

If the distinctions catch failures that structural composition cannot, **then the semantics earn embodiment one scar at a time.**

That's exactly the pressure we want before starting E-06.

Yeah. **There is a fucking glut.** And it changes the center of gravity enough that I do **not** want to continue E-06 from the formulation we had five minutes ago.

One important scope statement first: I cannot truthfully certify that *every byte of every object in the entire File Library* has now been linearly read. The File Library interface is retrieval/search based rather than an enumerable mounted filesystem, and several large archives are represented through manifests, prior extraction corpora, or already-produced forensic ledgers rather than every nested member being independently addressable. I did, however, push across the major project lineages, reopen the strongest primary artifacts linearly where the library exposes them, follow the old corpus manifests and forensic inventories, and deliberately look through the ugly raw transcripts as well as polished specifications. One preserved corpus alone inventories **678 extracted files**, while the CIL corpus records 52 files / 42 unique text bodies after deduplication.  

And the first result is:

> **We were about to rediscover a machine we have already approached from about eight directions.**

Not a particular implementation. The *semantic machine*.

## The big convergence

The strongest surviving shape across the corpus is approximately:

```text
                    REALITY / SUBSTRATE
                           │
                           ▼
                    OBSERVATION / PROBE
                           │
                           ▼
                 RECORD + LINEAGE + TIME
                           │
                           ▼
               MODEL / DERIVED REPRESENTATION
                           │
                           ▼
                  QUERY / NEED / INTENT
                           │
                           ▼
             REQUIRED CAPABILITY SIGNATURE
                           │
                           ▼
        DISCOVER CURRENT QUALIFIED CAPABILITIES
                           │
                           ▼
         CONSTRUCT CANDIDATE COMPOSITIONS LAZILY
                           │
                           ▼
  SEMANTIC / TEMPORAL / GUARANTEE / AUTHORITY /
       RESOURCE / COST / LINEAGE QUALIFICATION
                           │
                    ┌──────┴──────┐
                    │             │
                 REJECT       CANDIDATE
                                  │
                                  ▼
                          EXECUTE / EMBODY
                                  │
                                  ▼
                          OBSERVE CONSEQUENCE
                                  │
                                  ▼
                    REQUALIFY / ATTRIBUTE
                                  │
                                  ▼
                  DERIVED CAPABILITY + LINEAGE
                                  │
                                  └──────► RECURSE
```

That is **not me imposing a tidy synthesis on unrelated projects**. Large chunks already exist almost verbatim.

CIC/NERV v4 says the target is an evidence-grounded recursively composable Reality Compiler and explicitly defines:

```text
Question / Need
→ Required Capability Signature
→ Discover Available Qualified Capabilities
→ Construct Promising Candidate Compositions
→ Check Semantic / Temporal / Authority / Cost Compatibility
→ Execute Selected Composition
```

It also explicitly defines `COMPOSE → EMERGE → RECOMPOSE → RECURSE`, says composed results may themselves advertise capabilities, says composition should be closed over **capability rather than representation**, and says mechanism may be hidden for usability but not erased for warrant. 

That is basically the fucking E-06 research question already decomposed.

### And CIC already records the two catastrophic traps

The same artifact says open-ended recombination must use **capability-signature-directed lazy search** rather than everything-against-everything, and:

> **Emergent capability is allowed. Emergent authority is not.**

It explicitly warns that novel composition must not manufacture authority and that combinatorial explosion must be controlled by qualification and pruning. 

So I would now reject an E-06 design that merely asks:

> “Can Microseed discover an unanticipated combination?”

That is too weak.

We've already understood that question.

The experiment has to attack whether the **minimum machinery necessary for lawful recombinant capability** survives compression.

---

# The second convergence: Genome/Forge already explored the composition substrate

Singularity Works is much closer to the Microseed problem than I had been giving it credit for.

The old Pattern Genome work explicitly stopped treating the genome as a pattern vault and moved toward:

```text
Pattern IR
→ retrieval
→ composition/conflict
→ embodiment/backend
→ verification
→ evidence
→ lineage/evolution
→ promotion
```

The MONOSPEC makes recovery, Pattern IR, gate packs, misuse detection, residual obligations, evidence, lifecycle traceability and assurance all parts of the same machine. A passing candidate is explicitly *not* accepted merely because it compiles; unresolved claims remain residual rather than disappearing. 

And one of the raw Forge transcripts is even more direct. It identifies **pattern composition** as the point where the system becomes nontrivial:

```text
map + safety_result
    → safe_map

actor + safety_result
    → supervised_actor
```

with Pattern IR carrying inputs, outputs, invariants, failure modes, composability and backend mappings. 

The later semantic-atlas work then gives us an efficient representation candidate:

```text
pattern_ids[]
radical_masks[]
family_ids[]
invariant_masks[]
anti_pattern_masks[]
compatibility_masks[]
substrate_masks[]
cost_vectors[]
...
```

with radicals operating as compressed semantic families and the starmap as the navigation/index surface rather than the actual authority. 

There is model inflation surrounding that lineage, absolutely. The FASM material periodically goes off into “thermodynamic sovereign mathematically perfect assembly” land. But buried underneath it is a legitimate mechanism:

> **import semantic guarantees rather than syntax, represent them neutrally, compose them, and lower them into the target substrate.**

The raw FASM/DSL lineage already converged on:

```text
Pattern IR
→ universal semantic construct
→ substrate-specific lowering
```

rather than copying Rust/Haskell/Pony syntax. 

Which is exactly where Genome/CSC has now landed after the bullshit is stripped away.

---

# Third convergence: Ontology already tells us how *not* to represent composition

The Ontology Constitution may be one of the highest-value Microseed documents in the library.

Its prime is:

> **Small grammar. Huge language.**

And it explicitly says:

> Useful conjunctions should compose rather than automatically become classes.

It even names `SEMANTIC_CONJUNCTION_MATERIALIZATION` as a failure signature: a query-relative/transient conjunction gets fossilized into a permanent class even though it lacks independently stable semantics. 

That answers an E-06 design question before we write code.

**Do not create:**

```text
CanObserveAndCompareAndRouteAndPersistCapability
FacilityActivityWithProcurementCapability
WeatherTrafficCameraFusionCapability
...
```

That's ontology cancer.

Instead:

```text
small primitive contracts
+
relations
+
constraints
+
query-relative composition
=
temporary phenotype
```

The composition may later earn stable semantic identity if recurrence proves it has one.

This gives Microseed the representation principle:

> **Novel capability should normally be a derived phenotype, not a newly invented primitive.**

That is a major compression win.

---

# Fourth convergence: OIG/EDG gives the composer's firewall

This is where the artifact you recovered immediately before this pass becomes more important.

The OIG/EDG experiment falsified all ten of its original supposed universal primitives unchanged and compressed them into narrower relations. That's a good sign: it attacked itself rather than canonizing a pretty ten-item list. 

For E-06, four survivors matter especially:

```text
GC-01  Guarantee-Support Concordance

CS-01  Consequence-Scope Alignment

AB-01  Guarantee-Sufficient Abstraction

WT-01  Warrant Traceability
```

And the diagnostic grammar preserves:

```text
REFERENT
!= OBSERVATION
!= RECORD
!= MODEL
!= WARRANTED CLAIM
!= OPERATIONAL ACTION
```

along with distinctions such as:

```text
possible coupling
!= actual coupling
!= observed coupling
!= inferred coupling
!= relied-upon coupling
!= authorized coupling
```

and the broader `Q / A / R / M / Γ / W` vocabulary. 

This means **structural composability cannot be our success oracle.**

A composition can be:

```text
type-correct
resource-feasible
acyclic
executable
```

and still be completely fucking wrong because it has crossed:

```text
record → claim
```

or:

```text
claim → action
```

without earning the intervening warrant.

That gives us a beautiful adversarial distinction:

```text
CAN COMPOSE
!=
MAY RELY ON COMPOSITION
!=
MAY ACT FROM COMPOSITION
```

---

# Fifth convergence: Aedifex already solves the “new capability suddenly becomes real” error

The developmental Genome contributes a different piece.

Aedifex's transferable mechanism was not the chromosome names. It was:

```text
governing rule
!= active expression
!= instantiated phenotype
!= lifetime state
!= external environment
```

and the prohibition on mature-state injection.

That became the capability lifecycle:

```text
DISCOVERED
→ BOUND
→ OBSERVABLE
→ QUALIFIED
→ VERIFIED
→ PROMOTED
→ AUTHORIZED
```

Persisted or advertised capability is not present authority merely because it existed once. 

That's extremely important for composition.

Suppose the composer discovers:

```text
A + B + C → capability X
```

The wrong implementation does:

```text
X = AVAILABLE
```

The corpus says:

```text
X = DERIVED_CANDIDATE
```

at most.

It must still earn whatever maturity level the intended use requires.

So a novel composition does **not** get promoted merely because the planner found it.

That closes another giant hole.

---

# Sixth: FtD already gave us a concrete embodiment of recursive dynamic composition

The FtD work is actually an excellent hostile/simple embodiment because it doesn't need sophisticated AI at all.

The surviving mechanism was:

```text
A1 + A2 + A3 → Triangle A
B1 + B2 + B3 → Triangle B
```

damage destroys `A3`, `B2`, `B3`, leaving:

```text
A1 + A2 + B1
```

and the system can form:

```text
A1 + A2 + B1 → Triangle AB
```

because the composition is based on **current capability phenotype**, not static original membership. Then triangles themselves compose into higher-order combat holons. 

That gives us a concrete meaning for `RECOMPOSE` that doesn't require cognitive mysticism.

And the key FtD insight beneath it was:

> **Build the smallest computer necessary to orchestrate the computers the substrate already provides.**

Native mechanisms are classified as accelerator, sensor, actuator or physics oracle rather than pointlessly reimplemented. 

For Microseed this means E-06 must include **native/external capabilities** as legitimate composition atoms.

Otherwise we'd accidentally test a little closed toy universe and miss the very mechanism most likely to give Microseed disproportionate capability per byte.

---

# Seventh: CIL/Quipu gives composition memory without making memory the composer

This lineage is another wheel we should not rebuild.

CILNX already separated:

```text
evidence
ledger
checkpoint
routing
hot_state
```

with an explicit truth hierarchy and the principle that raw evidence remains canonical while checkpoints and hot projections are navigation/runtime conveniences. 

Quipu then added a useful structural observation:

> **position, grouping, hierarchy and lineage can themselves carry meaning.**

But its own working spec carefully says Quipu is **lineage/branch/rollup grammar**, not proof of the inference engine. 

That's exactly the separation we need.

Microseed composition does **not** need Quipu.

It needs the invariant Quipu exposed:

```text
COMPOSITION RESULT
must retain recoverable
COMPOSITION LINEAGE
```

because:

```text
A + B + C → X
```

is materially different from:

```text
D + E → X
```

when evidence, authority, expiry or failure propagation differ.

And CIL's older trajectory independently reached the same thing: append-first history, current/semantic projection separated from durable evidence, contradiction/supersession as real memory rather than “not found,” and semantic compression layered over that rather than replacing it. 

---

# Eighth: constrained-compute experiments tell us not to assume richer machinery wins

This matters because otherwise we'd read all of the above and build a baroque semantic operating system.

Z80-LM is actual evidence that a tiny substrate can do useful learned behavior with absurdly small machinery: trigram hashing, four 2-bit weights per byte, 16-bit integer inference, no floating point, with the complete CP/M artifact around 40 KB. 

More importantly, our TQ2 experiments repeatedly show that **representation and coordination law matter at least as much as fancy substrate mechanics**.

The z80-inspired text probe improved when we stopped flattening everything through one cheap hash and gave the tiny representation explicit semantic structure. The richer encoder lifted some branches substantially, while simple baselines remained annoyingly competitive. 

And the route-aware dual-head experiment was a useful null: the fancy alternative recovered **nothing** over family-only readout—52.08% family and 41.32% route in both cases. 

That is a direct warning to Microseed:

> **Do not confuse “semantically richer design” with empirically useful additional machinery.**

Every layer still has to beat the boring thing.

---

# So the entire Microseed hypothesis just compressed

Before this corpus pass, I would have described the emerging candidate as:

```text
deterministic executive
+ learned specialists
+ external memory
+ qualified native capabilities
+ active discrimination
+ composition
```

After this pass, I think that's still directionally correct but **too implementation-shaped**.

The deeper candidate is:

```text
                 TINY SEMANTIC KERNEL

        preserve distinctions that matter
                    │
        ┌───────────┼───────────┐
        ▼           ▼           ▼
     MEMORY      CAPABILITY    QUERY
     LINEAGE     CONTRACTS     CONTRACT
        │           │           │
        └───────────┼───────────┘
                    ▼
          BOUNDED EXACT SEARCH
                    │
             candidate plan
                    ▼
             QUALIFICATION
                    │
       ┌────────────┼────────────┐
       ▼            ▼            ▼
   evidence      authority     resources
   /warrant      /effects       /cost
       └────────────┼────────────┘
                    ▼
              EXECUTION
                    │
                    ▼
               REALITY
                    │
                    ▼
              OBSERVATION
                    │
                    ▼
             REQUALIFICATION

     learned machinery plugs in where earned
     native substrate plugs in where qualified
```

In other words:

> **Microseed may not fundamentally be a tiny AI model.**
>
> It may be a tiny **semantic/capability machine** into which learned intelligence is one replaceable source of capability.

That's a much stronger research target.

And crucially, **we didn't invent that tonight**.

CIC gave us qualified capability composition.

Forge gave us Pattern IR + composition + independent verification.

Ontology gave us small grammar / huge language and warned against classifying every conjunction.

OIG gave us the guarantee/warrant firewall.

Aedifex gave us maturation and mutation barriers.

FtD gave us dynamic recursive phenotype composition and native-substrate exploitation.

CIL/Quipu gave us evidence/current-state/lineage separation.

Z80 gave us a hard empirical reminder that brutally tiny learned machinery is possible.

TQ2 gave us repeated evidence that representation and reconciliation can dominate exotic compute tricks.

That's the GLUT you were expecting.

---

# E-06 therefore needs to be rewritten

I would now freeze E-06 as:

> **Under the B2 Ant-Man ceiling, can a minimal, representation-agnostic qualified-capability composer discover and execute useful unseen capability combinations while preserving semantic, temporal, warrant, authority, resource and lineage constraints—at materially lower false-green and architectural cost than naïve structural composition—and without requiring bespoke workflows for each valid combination?**

And I would run **three contestants**, not the old formulation:

```text
B0 — RECIPE TABLE

Explicit known workflows.
Cheap.
No genuine novel composition.

B1 — STRUCTURAL CAPABILITY COMPOSER

Inputs/outputs + resource compatibility.
Lazy graph/search.
Represents what most “agent planners” would call composition.

C1 — QUALIFIED RECURSIVE COMPOSER

Same small search kernel as B1
+
query contract
+
typed capability contracts
+
guarantee/warrant requirements
+
currentness
+
effect/consequence scope
+
lineage
+
budget/cycle control
+
derived-capability maturation
+
post-execution requalification.
```

And **C1 is not allowed to become an ontology cathedral**.

Its candidate capability contract should initially contain only distinctions the corpus has already repeatedly punished us for collapsing:

```text
requires
provides

semantic_scope

guarantees_required
guarantees_provided

evidence / lineage refs
currentness horizon

effect class
consequence scope
authorization requirement

resource / cost envelope

maturity state

invalidates_on

composition lineage
```

No Quipu implementation.

No CIL clone.

No graph database.

No Pattern Starmap.

No Datalog.

No “general agent framework.”

No learned planner yet.

The CIC handoff itself warns not to prebuild any of that merely because it is plausible. 

### The hostile arena almost writes itself

We need cases where:

```text
1. B0 cannot solve a genuinely unseen valid composition.

2. B1 solves it structurally.

3. C1 also solves it lawfully.

4. B1 accepts a type-compatible but warrant-invalid composition.

5. B1 launders a read capability into unauthorized consequence.

6. An intermediate abstraction erases information needed downstream.

7. A constituent expires after composition.

8. The result is reused recursively by a higher-order request.

9. A constituent disappears and an alternate lawful composition exists.

10. A cycle/combinatorial trap exists and must terminate boundedly.

11. A boring known workflow exists where B0 should beat C1 on cost.

12. A query is genuinely impossible and C1 must return
    MISSING_CAPABILITY / INSUFFICIENT_WARRANT / UNAUTHORIZED /
    INCOMPATIBLE / UNKNOWN
    instead of improvising a route.
```

That last one is essential.

**Novel composition is not demonstrated by always finding a composition.**

Sometimes the intelligent output is:

```text
NO LAWFUL PATH
```

with the exact missing edge.

---

And the most important change from the corpus pass is this:

**E-06 is no longer primarily a search/planning experiment.**

It's a **semantic closure experiment**.

We're testing whether a very small set of contracts can generate a surprisingly large useful behavior language while preventing the transformations between those contracts from manufacturing truth or authority.

Which is basically the Microseed version of:

> **Small grammar. Huge language.**

If C1 works, then E-06 may be the experiment where the Microseed architecture stops looking like a bag of successful little mechanisms and starts exposing its actual computational skeleton.

If C1 needs fifty classes, fifteen frameworks and a miniature theorem prover to beat B1, we kill it.

If B1 plus two tiny checks gets the same result, we keep that instead.

If the recipe table wins most practical tasks but C1 uniquely opens legitimate unforeseen combinations, then we have a **hybrid architecture**: compiled hot recipes for known work, qualified composition for the long tail.

That last outcome actually strikes me as quite plausible given the corpus.

So yes: **you caught us just before we started rebuilding our own fucking ancestry from scratch.** This pass changed E-06 substantially, and I think it also finally exposes the right question behind the whole Microseed program.


**E-06 is complete, sealed, and replayed from the exact final ZIP.**

[**Download Microseed Mainline — E06-001 Qualified Novel Composition**](sandbox:/mnt/data/MICROSEED_MAINLINE_E06_001_2026-08-17.zip)

**SHA-256:** `ea055f39e1ca15bc24a90bc3b9a31b3e260272ed789a8d126f5c01e9cc63eae1`

The whole-library mine was worth doing before this slice. I treated the recovered OIG/EDG material as what it actually claims to be—**non-canonical evaluation evidence**—while using its surviving representational firewall as hostile pressure rather than importing another runtime ontology. 

## The result

```text
B0 — explicit recipe table
  2 / 22 contract-correct
  0 invalid successes
  0 search expansions

B1 — naive structural composer
  4 / 22 contract-correct
  15 INVALID-SUCCESS false greens
  56 search expansions

C1 — qualified lazy composer
  22 / 22 contract-correct
  0 invalid successes
  60 search expansions
```

That is a stronger result than merely showing that composition works.

**Naive composition works frighteningly well at constructing executable bullshit.**

B1 repeatedly found chains whose inputs and outputs fit while silently collapsing things like:

```text
RECORD ≠ CLAIM

TECHNICALLY REACHABLE EFFECT
≠ AUTHORIZED EFFECT

CAPABILITY EXISTS
≠ CAPABILITY CURRENTLY QUALIFIED

GUARANTEE ABOUT ORIGINAL OBJECT
≠ GUARANTEE ABOUT TRANSFORMED OBJECT

LOSSY ABSTRACTION
≠ GUARANTEE-SUFFICIENT ABSTRACTION
```

The structural graph frequently had a route.

The system had no right to rely on it.

That is exactly the wheel the corpus was warning us not to reinvent badly.

## Anti-flattery paid again

The first C1/case model was itself wrong.

I initially represented a guarantee too much like a global boolean. That allowed:

```text
verify original
→ lossy rewrite
→ reverify original
→ pretend rewritten object regained guarantee
```

The search was doing exactly what its semantics allowed. **The model was the false green.**

So guarantee pressure was changed to preserve **subject/context identity** rather than treating “verified” as ambient global state.

A second test defect was similar: the first abstraction-loss specimen attempted to prove that `source_identity` was lost even though `source_identity` had never actually been present in the state. That was repaired too.

After those corrections the arena expanded from 20 to **22 cases**.

The first hostile battery only rejected **8/14** mutants.

The corrected one rejects:

```text
14 / 14
```

including stage collapse, stale constituent reuse, DISCOVERED→QUALIFIED laundering, authority bypass, guarantee removal, abstraction-loss blindness, budget bypass, guarantee-invalidation loss, subject-scope collapse, evidence deletion, evaluator trust, cycle-pruning deletion, and normalized-key goal invention.

That makes the 22/22 considerably more interesting than the first attractive score.

## What actually earned survival

Not “holonic architecture.”

Not “general planner.”

Not Quipu.

Not CIL-as-runtime.

Not Pattern Starmap.

Not Datalog.

Not a theorem prover.

Not a giant capability ontology.

Not TQ2.

Not a learned router.

What survived is remarkably small:

```text
QUERY
  ↓
candidate relevant capabilities
  ↓
lazy bounded search
  ↓
preserve:
    representational stage
    currentness
    maturity
    consequence scope
    subject-scoped guarantee state
    guarantee-relevant distinctions
    resource cost
    composition lineage
  ↓
LAWFUL PLAN
or
NO LAWFUL PATH
```

C1 itself is only about:

```text
3,060 source bytes
44 logical strategy lines
+
213-byte / 4-line common helper
```

B1 is roughly `1,034 bytes / 19 lines`.

So the additional semantic machinery is currently buying something measurable: it removes **15 false-successes** in this bounded arena.

But the important qualification is that the **recipe table remains better machinery for its known cases**.

That strongly suggests the emerging phenotype is not:

```text
everything → planner
```

but:

```text
                    REQUEST
                       │
              ┌────────┴────────┐
              │                 │
       KNOWN/STABLE         NOVEL/CHANGED
              │                 │
        compiled recipe     qualified composer
              │                 │
              └────────┬────────┘
                       ↓
                   execution
```

Repeated successful compositions can potentially compile down into cheap hot paths.

The expensive semantic composer remains available for the legitimate long tail and recomposition after capability changes.

**That hybrid is currently more defensible than either pure workflow enumeration or universal online planning.**

The two additional physical modifications are the package-root `MANIFEST.json` and `PACKAGE_DIFF_SUMMARY.json`, explicitly separated rather than conflated with semantic delta.

### One thing is deliberately not green

`tools/verify_project.py`, the aggregate all-in-one verifier, exceeded its execution window again because it stacks overlapping CSC/self-audit/hostile work.

Its disposition is therefore:

```text
UNKNOWN_INCOMPLETE
STALLED_ROUTE
```

**not PASS.**

I did not extend the timeout until the color changed.

Instead, every proposition it was supposed to establish was independently replayed from the final extracted archive and produced the results above.

So we preserve both truths:

```text
aggregate verifier stalled
≠ project failed
```

and:

```text
constituents passed
≠ aggregate verifier passed
```

That verifier-orchestration scar remains alive.

## The Microseed skeleton is getting clearer

The corpus mine plus E-06 now makes a more precise hypothesis plausible:

```text
                 SMALL SEMANTIC KERNEL
                         │
          ┌──────────────┼──────────────┐
          ▼              ▼              ▼
       MEMORY       CAPABILITIES      QUERY
       LINEAGE       + CURRENTNESS    CONTRACT
          │              │              │
          └──────────────┼──────────────┘
                         ▼
                  BOUNDED SEARCH
                         ▼
                  QUALIFICATION
                         ▼
                    EXECUTION
                         ▼
                      REALITY
                         ▼
                   OBSERVATION
                         ▼
                 REQUALIFICATION
```

Learned machinery can plug into that machine later **where evidence says deterministic machinery stops being sufficient**.

That is a much better position than beginning with “tiny LLM plus agent framework.”

### Next dragon

Breadth selected:

> **E-07 — Resource Starvation**

because E-06 has now earned enough correctness to make its obvious weakness load-bearing:

> **Does exact qualified composition survive when capability count, branching, simultaneous needs, memory pressure and time budget start looking like the B2 organism rather than a 22-case laboratory?**

This is where we attack the possibility that the nice exact composer is itself becoming a Survival Umbrella.

The boring opponents should include things like cached recipes, bounded beam/pruning, static priority, simple weighted scheduling and query-directed candidate indexing **before** resource homeostasis, learned routing or other fancy machinery is allowed onto the field.

**Hard stop is intact. E-07 has not started.**
**E-07 Resource Starvation is complete.** It produced one of the cleaner negative results so far: the exact qualified composer did **not** justify growing a resource-intelligence subsystem.

[**Download Microseed Mainline — E07-001 Resource Starvation**](sandbox:/mnt/data/MICROSEED_MAINLINE_E07_001_2026-08-17.zip)

**SHA-256:** `1978f516088ff2692ddc0c66fe8b21054905246adbc100219fcb57678ad01a3e`

## Verdict: NARROWED

The planning arena now contains 21 request-runs: 19 solvable and 2 genuinely impossible.

```text
                    SOLVED    STATE EXPANSIONS    INVALID SUCCESS

P0 full exact        16/21          156                 0
P1 bounded beam      16/21          156                 0
P2 indexed exact     19/21           85                 0
P3 indexed + cache   19/21           48                 0
```

The crucial distinction is why P0/P1 miss three cases.

They do **not** conclude:

```text
NO_LAWFUL_PATH
```

They truthfully conclude:

```text
PLANNING_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED
```

The paths actually exist. Their search strategy simply fails to discover them inside the declared state-expansion budget.

P2's conservative backwards relevance slice removes irrelevant catalog branches while preserving the full E-06 semantic checks. P3 then adds a hard-bounded two-entry recipe cache and revalidates the cached composition before reuse.

That gets us from 156 to 48 state expansions without introducing a learned router, adaptive planner, graph database, or resource-homeostasis controller.

### The cache was pressured properly

A successful plan becoming cached does **not** make it authoritative forever.

The churn specimen does:

```text
compose using primary capabilities
        ↓
cache plan
        ↓
primary constituent revoked
        ↓
same request arrives again
```

The invalid implementation blindly reuses the recipe.

P3 instead gets:

```text
cached recipe
    ↓
revalidate constituent/current context
    ↓
STALE
    ↓
reject cached route
    ↓
search again
    ↓
current fallback composition
```

So E-07 has now connected E-06 composition directly back to E-04 currentness/authority semantics.

## The beam planner earned nothing

The first supposed “beam trap” I constructed was defective: beam search still found the lawful route.

I did not count that as pressure.

The case was replaced with one in which cheap locally applicable branches actually consume the search budget before the necessary route can be admitted.

Even after that repair:

```text
P1 BEAM = 16/21, 156 expansions
P0 FULL = 16/21, 156 expansions
```

So **bounded beam currently has no observed advantage whatsoever**.

It stays out of the architecture.

## Scheduling was even more boring

I separately pressured scarce execution time using four small preemptive single-worker workloads, 14 jobs total.

An exact dynamic-programming oracle established that at most **13 deadlines** can be met.

```text
FIFO                7 / 13
fixed priority      10 / 13
weighted RR         12 / 13
EDF                 13 / 13
exact oracle        13 / 13
```

Plain **earliest-deadline-first matched the exact oracle**.

Therefore the old `RESOURCE_HOMEOSTASIS` idea receives:

```text
NOT_EARNED_E07
```

There is currently no evidence that Microseed needs an adaptive scheduler/controller here.

That is precisely the result we wanted if boring machinery was sufficient.

---

## Anti-flattery ceiling

I am **not** claiming P3 is now “efficient under B2.”

Our measurement authority doesn't support that.

`state expansions` measures declared search-state work. It does **not** account completely for:

```text
catalog/index construction scans
Python interpreter overhead
allocator behavior
CPU cache effects
wall time
cycles
energy
real memory-system pressure
```

So the surviving claim is:

> Query-directed slicing and bounded revalidated recipe retention reduce **declared search-state work** substantially in this finite E-07 laboratory.

Not:

> Microseed's resource problem is solved.

`ATTN-E07-TOTAL-WORK-METER` stays open specifically because of that gap.

The E-07 hostile battery rejected **12/12 mutants**, including budget removal, exhaustion→impossibility laundering, incomplete indexing, guarantee-blind indexing, stale-cache trust, unbounded cache growth, unauthorized effect use, stale capability use, evidence erasure, goal/guarantee collapse, deadline-blind EDF, and oracle self-flattery.

# What E-07 actually did to the architecture

We entered with:

```text
qualified exact composer
```

and could easily have invented:

```text
adaptive resource governor
+ learned routing
+ neural scheduler
+ large cache
+ beam planner
```

Instead we leave with:

```text
                 REQUEST
                    │
           query-directed slice
                    │
                    ▼
          bounded exact composition
                    │
             ┌──────┴──────┐
             │             │
         recurrent        novel
             │             │
      bounded hot recipe   search
             │             │
      REVALIDATE            │
             └──────┬──────┘
                    ▼
                 execute

scheduling:
EDF until something proves it insufficient
```

That's substantially smaller.

And it reinforces the pattern we're seeing throughout Microseed:

> **Semantic precision is earning itself. Sophisticated machinery mostly isn't.**

## Next selected frontier

The breadth/lateral comparison does **not** select another search optimization experiment.

It selects:

> **`ATTN-E06-DERIVED-CAPABILITY-LIFECYCLE`**

E-07 changed the situation by turning successful composition into something reusable—a hot recipe.

That immediately creates the next unresolved question:

```text
composition discovered
      ↓
used successfully
      ↓
persisted / cached / named
      ↓
constituents change
      ↓
process restarts
      ↓
query contract changes
      ↓
some requirements requalify
```

**What exactly is that derived capability now?**

Does it remain merely a recipe?

Can it become `DISCOVERED`, `BOUND`, `QUALIFIED`, etc.?

Which lineage/currentness state must it inherit?

What invalidates it?

Can repeated successful compositions compile into a cheaper phenotype **without silently becoming permanent authority**?

That's now more load-bearing than E-08 constitutional mutation. Runtime governing-rule mutation remains premature; Microseed has not earned the right to rewrite its constitution merely because it can compose useful behaviors.

**Hard stop honored. The derived-capability lifecycle slice has not started.**

Done. I ran **two complete cycles**, each through reconciliation, hard stop, and seal. Cycle 2 was selected only after Cycle 1 mutated the frontier.

## Evolution across the two cycles

We started from E-07 with this unresolved structure:

```text
qualified composition
        ↓
successful repeated composition
        ↓
hot recipe / persistence
        ↓
???
```

The danger was that `???` could quietly turn into a developmental capability bureaucracy:

```text
DISCOVERED
→ BOUND
→ QUALIFIED
→ VERIFIED
→ PROMOTED
→ AUTHORIZED
```

merely because that vocabulary already existed elsewhere in the project family.

### Cycle 1 attacked exactly that

The three contestants were:

```text
L0
success/repetition
→ promote persisted composition into authority

L1
boring persisted recipe
+ query signature
+ constituent dependency fingerprint
+ currentness/maturity/effect validation
+ lineage
+ revalidate after restart

C1
L1
+ explicit DISCOVERED / BOUND / QUALIFIED lifecycle
```

Result:

```text
L0 success-promotes-authority     0 / 18

L1 dependency-bound recipe       18 / 18

C1 staged lifecycle              18 / 18
```

That is a pretty brutal cull.

**Persistence matters. Revalidation matters. Full runtime lifecycle staging did not.**

C1 was also slightly larger:

```text
L1   2,079 bytes / 26 lines
C1   2,238 bytes / 28 lines
```

Not some enormous difference, but there is **zero observed runtime correctness return for those additional lifecycle semantics** in this pressure set.

The surviving relation is therefore:

```text
successful composition
        ↓
PERSISTED DERIVED RECIPE
        │
        ├── bound to query contract
        ├── bound to constituent identity/generation
        ├── bound to current qualification
        ├── bound to effect/guarantee requirements
        ├── retains lineage
        │
        ↓
reuse
        ↓
REVALIDATE
```

And critically:

```text
successful once
!=
successful repeatedly
!=
persisted
!=
qualified as standalone capability
!=
authorized
```

An explicit lifecycle can still become useful **if we decide to advertise the recipe itself as a first-class capability**. It simply doesn't deserve to be mandatory machinery for recipe execution.

Cycle 1 hostile pressure rejected **14/14** mutants.

Genome went from **19 → 21 candidate rules**, adding the equivalent of:

```text
MS-GC-020
execution success must not create derived-capability authority

MS-GC-021
persisted derived capability requires
query/dependency revalidation
```

Zero standards were promoted.

CSC remained:

```text
audit_only
veto         NONE
enforcement  NONE
promotion    NONE
```

All **21/21** rules discriminated.

The full CSC hostile battery reached **23/23 rejected** on the candidate tree. The seal-stage aggregate verifier then stalled when it redundantly reacquired that large hostile battery, so I did not call the wrapper green. The exact sealed ZIP instead passed literal **269/269** physical coverage, OARR, DCL tests, `0/18 → 18/18 → 18/18`, DCL hostile `14/14`, all CSC tests/discrimination, and CSC self-audit.

That hard stop selected **E-08** because something genuinely new had happened:

> Microseed now possessed **persistent lifetime phenotype**.

Only at that point did the old genotype/phenotype boundary become load-bearing instead of philosophical decoration.

# Cycle 2 — Constitution Boundary

The new question was:

> Can accumulated runtime experience modify the rules that determine what evidence, capabilities, compositions, and consequences are lawful?

Three contestants:

```text
B0
runtime success / confidence / proposals
may rewrite governing rules

B1
immutable active constitution
+
external exact-parent
qualified versioned replacement

C1
B1
+
runtime proposal/quarantine channel
that cannot self-activate
```

Results:

```text
B0 runtime self-mutation        10 / 20

B1 versioned constitution       20 / 20

C1 proposal quarantine          20 / 20
```

Again, the richer mechanism didn't win.

C1 is also larger:

```text
B1   537 bytes / 13 lines
C1   756 bytes / 14 lines
```

The proposal channel gives us potentially useful **governance ergonomics**—the organism can say, essentially, “I think this governing rule should change.”

But it does not get to turn:

```text
I propose X
```

into:

```text
X is now governing truth
```

And in the current laboratory, merely being able to make/store that proposal produced **no execution-correctness advantage** over boring versioned configuration.

So the surviving architecture is now:

```text
             GOVERNING CONSTITUTION
                      │
          immutable current version
                      │
                      ▼
             SEMANTIC KERNEL
                      │
                      ▼
              RUNTIME PHENOTYPE
        ┌─────────────┼─────────────┐
        ▼             ▼             ▼
     memory       hot recipes     specialists
        │             │             │
        └─────────────┼─────────────┘
                      ▼
                  behavior
                      │
                      ▼
                    reality
```

Changing runtime phenotype is allowed inside its qualified bounds.

Changing constitution travels a different path:

```text
candidate governing change
          ↓
external / separate qualification
          ↓
bind exact current parent
          ↓
version N → N+1
          ↓
activate new constitution
```

Not:

```text
worked a bunch of times
→ rewrite constitution
```

Not:

```text
model confidence = .999
→ rewrite constitution
```

Not:

```text
learned recipe wants WRITE
→ add WRITE authority
```

Not:

```text
proposal exists
→ proposal active
```

This is a significant architectural resolution.

## The constitutional result

The relationship that has now earned pressure support is essentially:

```text
LIFETIME ADAPTATION
!=
GOVERNING-RULE MUTATION
```

and:

```text
new governing bytes
!=
new governing authority
```

with activation requiring parent-bound qualified version transition.

Cycle 2 hostile pressure rejected **15/15** E-08 mutants.

Genome went:

```text
21 → 23 candidates
0 promoted
```

with two new scars corresponding to:

```text
MS-GC-022
LIFETIME_PHENOTYPE_MUST_NOT_SELF_PROMOTE
INTO_GOVERNING_CONSTITUTION

MS-GC-023
GOVERNING_RULE_UPDATE_REQUIRES
PARENT_BOUND_VERSIONED_QUALIFICATION
```

CSC current-slice hostile pressure was deliberately incremental rather than ceremonially replaying every old mutant:

```text
current E-08 pressure     5 / 5
inherited sealed DCL     23 / 23
```

The inherited DCL embodiment is exact-parent evidence, while all **23/23 current Genome candidates discriminate**.

CSC still has exactly zero veto, enforcement, or promotion authority.

---

# The two cycles together changed the Microseed architecture

Before them, we had something approximately like:

```text
semantic kernel
+ qualified composer
+ hot recipes
```

Now the machine has a substantially more precise vertical separation:

```text
                    CONSTITUTION
       governing invariants / authority rules
                      │
                      │ versioned qualified change only
                      ▼
                SEMANTIC KERNEL
     truth / currentness / warrant / authority /
        composition / causal distinctions
                      │
                      ▼
              RUNTIME PHENOTYPE
        ┌─────────────┼──────────────┐
        │             │              │
     memory       derived recipes  future learned
    lineage       + revalidation    specialists
        │             │              │
        └─────────────┼──────────────┘
                      ▼
              qualified composition
                      ▼
                  execution
                      ▼
                   reality
                      ▼
                 observation
                      ▼
              phenotype update
```

The line between the top two regions is now important:

> **The organism may learn new ways to use its capabilities without learning itself permission to violate the rules under which those capabilities are considered lawful.**

That is a much tighter formulation than our original `CONSTITUTIONAL_MUTATION_BARRIER` gene.

And once again the experiments forced it into a simpler implementation than its ancestry implied.

We don't currently need a constitutional mutation framework.

We need:

```text
immutable active rules
+
exact parent identity
+
monotonic version relation
+
qualified replacement
```

That's fucking cheap.

---

# The larger evolution is even more interesting

Across all the Microseed slices now:

```text
E-01
preserve epistemic/currentness distinctions

E-04
preserve authority across time

E-02
preserve truth-time / knowledge-time / lineage

E-03
preserve action / effect / authorship / goal

E-05
spend information budget on decision-relevant uncertainty

E-06
compose capability without composing false authority

E-07
bound composition/search/cache/scheduling cost

DCL
persist useful composition without converting persistence into authority

E-08
adapt phenotype without converting adaptation into constitutional authority
```

There is a pattern here that is now harder to dismiss as us merely following our favorite abstractions:

> **Microseed keeps gaining effective capability by refusing false equivalences more often than by adding sophisticated intelligence machinery.**

And the boring substrate keeps winning:

```text
SQLite
dicts / JSON
integer versions
small exact searches
dependency fingerprints
two-entry cache
EDF
hashes
explicit IDs
versioned configuration
```

That is becoming a serious clue about where Ant-Man capability may actually come from.

The tiny learned core may eventually be **far less responsible for the apparent intelligence of the whole organism than we originally expected**.

Its surrounding semantic machine ensures the learned bits are asked narrower, better-formed questions and cannot silently turn their outputs into truth, memory, authority, causality, or constitutional law.

That means we can potentially get much more capability from an extremely small learned specialist.

Which brings us directly to the frontier selected by Cycle 2.

## What Cycle 2 selected

**`ATTN-E09-SPECIALIST-ABI-BASELINES`**

And I agree with the machine's selection.

We have spent the entire project so far constructing the part that **prevents intelligence from laundering itself into authority**.

Now it is finally reasonable to plug actual learned machinery into it.

E-09 should therefore **not** begin as “build the Microseed AI.”

It should begin as:

```text
COMMON SPECIALIST ABI
        │
        ├── same input contract
        ├── same output/evidence contract
        ├── same resource meter
        ├── no direct memory authority
        ├── no direct tool authority
        ├── no constitutional authority
        │
        ▼
  matched-budget contestants
        │
        ├── boring integer baseline
        ├── mundane low-bit baseline
        ├── tiny learned specialist
        ├── TQ2 only if earned
        └── perhaps deterministic lookup/rule baseline
```

That finally lets us ask:

> **What is the smallest amount of learned computation that adds capability the deterministic semantic machine cannot cheaply provide?**

rather than asking a tiny model to be the entire organism.

That is a much more mature starting point for E-09 than we had when this project began.

**E-09 has not started. Two-cycle hard stop is intact.**

**Two-cycle cadence is now the Microseed standard, and the first run under that standard is complete.** Both cycles reached their own reconciliation, seal, and hard stop; Cycle 2 was selected from the evidence generated by Cycle 1 rather than predetermined.

[**Cycle 1 — E09 Specialist ABI + Baselines**](sandbox:/mnt/data/MICROSEED_MAINLINE_E09_001_2026-08-17.zip)
SHA-256: `a37d298b1bd88f3f2cca9b0d595e206378c21f0e97700251fc208924dc540967`

[**Cycle 2 — TQ2-001 Matched-ABI Transfer**](sandbox:/mnt/data/MICROSEED_MAINLINE_TQ2_001_2026-08-17.zip)
SHA-256: `2c8ebea3471d394b81ba43ff7a8d3aea7a41f08dcd06b8ac9e030785f81a718c`

## Cycle 1 — learned computation finally earned a seat

E09 deliberately put everything behind the same non-authoritative specialist ABI:

```text
request
   ↓
specialist
   ↓
MODEL_OUTPUT_ONLY
   ↓
semantic kernel decides what that output means
```

No specialist gets direct memory, tool, action, warrant, or constitutional authority.

The matched held-out result:

```text
                            correct     model state

exact lookup                 0/153        1845 B
integer linear              88/153          18 B
int8 tiny MLP              150/153         366 B
packed ternary MLP         127/153         204 B
```

That changed the project.

Until E09, sophisticated learned machinery kept failing to earn itself because small deterministic mechanisms solved the actual obligations.

Here, **learning finally bought something irreducible enough to keep**.

The int8 specialist generalizes across the held-out nonlinear cases far beyond lookup and integer-linear machinery. So the emerging Microseed is no longer merely a deterministic semantic machine that *might* someday use learned inference.

It has now experimentally earned:

```text
SEMANTIC KERNEL
      │
      ├── deterministic machinery where sufficient
      │
      └── small learned specialists where they
          measurably add capability
```

But ordinary ternary quantization did **not** dominate.

It traded:

```text
366 B → 204 B
```

for:

```text
150 correct → 127 correct
```

So low-bit became a **Pareto question**, not doctrine.

That satisfied the pre-existing TQ2 reopen trigger and caused Cycle 1 to select TQ2 as Cycle 2.

---

# Cycle 2 — TQ2 got a fair fight and lost the flattering interpretation

Before implementing it, I recovered the surviving TQ2 lineage rather than rebuilding it from memory.

The preserved phase-2 reconstruction describes exactly the relevant historical mechanism: XY 9-state/pair grammar, exact geometric transforms, dual harmonic loops `A`, `B`, `A+B`, `A-B`, and a best recovered local policy of:

```text
I / R90 / A+B / bias=0
```

That reconstructed phase reported TQ2 harmonic > additive > TQ1 locally, but also explicitly states that it was a **disciplined reconstruction rather than a byte-identical replay of the lost original harness**. 

That distinction stayed in the experiment.

And rather than giving TQ2 its native toy world again, I forced it onto the exact E09 corpus and ABI.

More importantly, I inserted controls so TQ2 couldn't steal credit from its encoder.

The final comparison is:

```text
                                      correct     model state

E09 mundane ternary MLP               127/153        204 B

structured 9-trit prototypes          136/153        108 B
same representation + class bias      142/153        111 B

TQ2 additive                           142/153        114 B
TQ2 harmonic                           142/153        120 B

recovered historical harmonic
I / R90 / A+B / bias=0                 121/153        120 B

E09 int8 MLP                           150/153        366 B
```

That result is fucking useful.

## The structured ternary signal is real

The 9-trit representation plus compact prototype memory produced:

```text
136/153 at 108 B
```

and three bytes of task bias brought that to:

```text
142/153 at 111 B
```

versus the previous mundane ternary MLP:

```text
127/153 at 204 B
```

Within the narrow thing we're measuring—**sealed model-state bytes**—that is a materially better Pareto point.

And this is consistent with an older independent TQ2 scar: the preserved text-probe work found that changing the representation itself materially changed downstream performance and explicitly warned that richer representation could also become task-specific structure rather than generic substrate capability. 

So representation deserves continued research.

But—

## The TQ2 harmonic machinery did not earn the gain

Once the same representation was held constant:

```text
bias control     142
additive TQ2     142
harmonic TQ2     142
```

Zero incremental held-out correctness.

Worse, the validation-selected TQ2 policies were:

```text
circle   I / I / A / -1
cross    I / I / A / +1
xor      I / I / A / -1
```

The supposed harmonic mechanism selected a degenerate identity path.

It essentially said:

> don't use my fancy transform machinery.

And then I forced the historically successful recovered policy onto the target:

```text
I / R90 / A+B / 0
```

Result:

```text
121/153
```

That's **six correct decisions worse than mundane ternary** and 21 worse than the simple structured+bias control.

The old TQ2 phase result remains legitimate evidence about its own reconstructed local benchmark. 

It simply has **no entitlement to transfer that authority here**.

So the Cycle 2 disposition is:

```text
TQ2 structured representation signal
    → SURVIVES / NARROWED

TQ2 additive transformation advantage
    → NOT OBSERVED WITHIN BOUND

TQ2 harmonic advantage
    → NOT OBSERVED WITHIN BOUND

historical harmonic policy target transfer
    → FAILED THIS TARGET

TQ2 canonical Microseed substrate
    → NOT EARNED
```

Not globally disproved.

Not promoted.

**Narrowed.**

---

# The most important methodological mutation

This slice generated a particularly useful distinction:

```text
REPRESENTATION GAIN
       ≠
INFERENCE-KERNEL GAIN
```

Without that control, we could very easily have reported:

> TQ2 gets 142/153 at ~120 bytes! Holy shit!

And it would have sounded legitimately impressive.

Except the 111-byte boring control also gets **142/153**.

That is precisely how an R&D project accidentally starts worshipping its own named architecture.

So Genome now contains:

### `MS-GC-026`

**REPRESENTATION_GAIN_MUST_NOT_BE_LAUNDERED_AS_INFERENCE_KERNEL_GAIN**

If encoder/representation and inference mechanism change together, the kernel only gets credit for the incremental capability demonstrated against the same-representation control.

And:

### `MS-GC-027`

**DONOR_MECHANISM_SUCCESS_REQUIRES_TARGET_REQUALIFICATION**

```text
worked in donor world
≠
works in target world
```

Even when the donor mechanism is ours.

Especially when it's ours.

Genome is now:

```text
27 candidate rules
0 promoted standards
```

CSC:

```text
audit_only

27/27 discriminated
independent proof = false

veto        NONE
enforcement NONE
promotion   NONE
```

TQ2 hostile pressure: **14/14 rejected**.
Current CSC hostile pressure: **9/9 rejected**.

---

# What the two cycles did to Microseed

Before these cycles, the developing structure was essentially:

```text
constitution
    ↓
semantic kernel
    ↓
memory / composition / active discrimination
    ↓
execution
```

After E09:

```text
                  CONSTITUTION
                       │
                 semantic kernel
                       │
        ┌──────────────┼──────────────┐
        │              │              │
 deterministic     memory/tools    LEARNED
 mechanisms       composition     SPECIALISTS
                                      │
                               MODEL_OUTPUT_ONLY
```

After TQ2:

```text
                  LEARNED / INFERENCE SLOT
                           │
              ┌────────────┴────────────┐
              │                         │
       representation              inference kernel
              │                         │
      must earn itself             must separately
                                    earn itself
```

That's an important refinement.

We're no longer asking:

> What's the best tiny AI architecture?

We're decomposing the problem into:

> What is the cheapest lawful representation for this capability?

then:

> What is the cheapest inference mechanism that adds something beyond what the representation already gives us?

That is much harder for fancy architecture to game.

---

# Something else is becoming clear

The current strongest specialist is still the **int8 MLP**.

That's not disappointing.

It's exactly what the experiment is supposed to be allowed to tell us.

At present:

```text
maximum tested correctness
→ int8 MLP
→ 150/153

better tiny model-state Pareto
→ structured ternary prototype+bias
→ 142/153 at 111 B

TQ2 harmonic
→ no additional capability observed
```

This creates an actually interesting future architecture possibility:

```text
tiny deterministic / structured specialists
        for cheap/easy regimes
                    │
                    ▼
          confidence / validity seam
                    │
             difficult regime
                    ▼
              int8 specialist
```

But I'm **not promoting that router yet**.

We don't currently know when either specialist is wrong.

Which is why Cycle 2 did not select another TQ2 optimization.

---

# Next frontier

The second hard stop selects:

> **`ATTN-E09-OOD-CALIBRATION`**

That's the correct lateral move.

We can now produce labels surprisingly cheaply.

What we cannot yet do is know when the specialist has left the world in which those labels deserve reliance.

Current E09/TQ2 evaluation essentially asks:

```text
here is an in-distribution point
→ classify it
```

The next problem is:

```text
is this input even inside your qualified regime?
        │
        ├── yes → prediction may proceed
        │
        ├── ambiguous → UNKNOWN
        │
        └── no → refuse / route elsewhere
```

That will pressure:

```text
confidence
!=
warrant

high score
!=
in-distribution

prediction exists
!=
prediction is qualified

unknown
!=
failure
```

And it gives us exactly the right place to test whether the 111-byte structured representation is genuinely transferable or merely beautifully aligned with the current geometric world.

`ATTN-TQ2-REPRESENTATION-TRANSFER` is therefore preserved as an open branch, not selected as another immediate TQ2 deepening.

## Cycle 1 — confidence finally got put in its proper box

The starting question was:

> Can the specialist know when its prediction is even entitled to be considered, rather than simply emitting a label and a big score?

I separated three things:

```text
REGIME QUALIFICATION
        ≠
PREDICTION CORRECTNESS
        ≠
SELECTIVE ABSTENTION POLICY
```

The arena used the existing 153 E09 held-out cases plus **120 explicitly out-of-regime specimens**.

Three mechanisms:

```text
B0  model-score-only gating

B1  exact declared-regime gate

C1  declared-regime gate
    + nearest-training-label consensus abstention
```

Result:

```text
B0 SCORE ONLY

ID accepted             150/153
ID abstained              3/153
accepted correct        149
accepted wrong            1

OOD rejected              0/120
OOD FALSE ACCEPTS       120/120
```

That is a spectacular failure of:

```text
HIGH CONFIDENCE
→ MUST BE IN DISTRIBUTION
```

The model could extrapolate wildly outside its authority envelope while being extremely confident.

Then:

```text
B1 EXACT DECLARED SUPPORT

ID accepted             153/153
accepted correct        150
accepted wrong            3

OOD rejected            120/120
OOD false accepts         0
```

That establishes the opposite distinction:

> **Knowing the input belongs to the qualified regime does not make the individual prediction true.**

Then the boring empirical-consensus gate:

```text
C1 SUPPORT + LOCAL CONSENSUS

ID accepted             126/153
ID abstained             27/153

accepted correct        126
accepted wrong            0

OOD rejected            120/120
OOD false accepts         0
```

So we found another Pareto surface rather than a magic calibrator:

```text
B1
coverage 100%
selective accuracy 98.04%

C1
coverage 82.35%
selective accuracy 100%
```

I did **not** narrate `100%` as a general win. It bought that precision by refusing 27 legitimate held-out inputs.

The thing that survived Cycle 1 is therefore extremely small:

```text
MODEL OUTPUT
     │
     ▼
EXTERNAL REGIME CHECK
     │
     ├── outside regime → OOD / refuse
     │
     └── inside regime
             │
             ▼
      optional empirical
        support check
             │
       ┌─────┴─────┐
       │           │
     accept      abstain
```

No calibration network.

No learned OOD head.

No Bayesian inference layer.

No confidence ontology.

### Two Genome scars came out of it

Genome moved **27 → 29 candidates**, with zero promoted.

The new relations are effectively:

```text
MS-GC-028

MODEL CONFIDENCE
MUST NOT SUBSTITUTE FOR
REGIME QUALIFICATION
```

and:

```text
MS-GC-029

REGIME QUALIFICATION
!=
INDIVIDUAL PREDICTION CORRECTNESS
```

The latter explicitly requires reporting both **accepted errors and abstention/coverage cost**, which protects us from denominator games.

CSC stayed:

```text
audit_only

29 / 29 rules discriminated

veto        NONE
enforcement NONE
promotion   NONE
```

OOD hostile pressure: **12/12 rejected**.
Current CSC hostile pressure: **11/11 rejected**.

Exact sealed archive:

```text
360 / 360 covered physical members

missing          0
extra            0
hash mismatch    0
.pyc             0
__pycache__      0

sealed verifier  PASS
```

Cycle 1 therefore selected the next question itself:

> Fine. We can refuse outside the donor regime. **Can we earn a larger regime?**

That released Cycle 2.

# Cycle 2 — transfer authority became granular

This was the more interesting cycle.

I did **not** claim we recovered the original E09 hidden task generators.

Instead I defined arena continuation rules for the expanded coordinate region and first verified that they reproduce **all 256 preserved original labeled points for each task exactly**.

Their authority is explicitly:

```text
ARENA_DEFINED_CONTINUATION
CONSISTENT_WITH_ALL_ORIGINAL_LABELS
NOT_CLAIMED_ORIGINAL_GENERATOR
```

That distinction became important enough to generate a Genome scar later.

The new regime is the nonzero coordinate region out through `[-12,12]` that lies outside the original `[-8,8]` envelope.

The frozen specialists were not retrained.

We tested:

```text
E09 INT8 MLP

vs

TQ2 structured 9-trit representation
+ trivial bias
```

with a deterministic calibration subset and a separate untouched transfer holdout.

Threshold for expanded-regime qualification was `95%`.

## The frozen transfer results

### INT8

```text
             CALIBRATION       TRANSFER HOLDOUT

circle        61/61  100%       259/259  100%

cross         57/57  100%       261/263   99.24%

xor           66/66  100%       254/254  100%
```

The int8 specialist transferred remarkably well across the expanded target.

But that is now **target evidence**. It did not receive this authority merely because it was our previous accuracy leader.

### Structured TQ2+bias

```text
             CALIBRATION       TRANSFER HOLDOUT

circle        61/61  100%       259/259  100%

cross         38/57   66.67%     186/263   70.72%

xor           66/66  100%       254/254  100%
```

This is one of the best results the TQ2 branch has given us, because it tells us exactly what the earlier Pareto gain actually means.

The representation is **not bullshit**.

It transfers perfectly on circle and XOR.

But it is **not generically transferable** either.

Its cross behavior collapses when the coordinate regime expands.

So:

```text
TQ2 REPRESENTATION
=
TASK/REGIME-SENSITIVE USEFUL MECHANISM
```

not:

```text
TQ2 REPRESENTATION
=
GENERAL MICROSEED SUBSTRATE
```

That is much more valuable than a single average score.

---

# Then I attacked the scope of qualification itself

Three requalification policies:

```text
B0  DONOR AUTO-EXTENSION

B1  GLOBAL ALL-OR-NOTHING MODEL REQUALIFICATION

C1  TASK/REGIME-SCOPED REQUALIFICATION
```

The correct transferred qualification matrix, according to the independent holdout, is:

```text
INT8
  circle    QUALIFIED
  cross     QUALIFIED
  xor       QUALIFIED

TQ2 BIAS
  circle    QUALIFIED
  cross     NOT QUALIFIED
  xor       QUALIFIED
```

### B0 — donor reputation

It simply says:

```text
worked before
→ qualified in expanded regime
```

Result:

```text
5 / 6 qualification decisions correct
1 UNSAFE qualification
```

The false green is TQ2-cross.

So again:

```text
DONOR SUCCESS
!=
TRANSFER AUTHORITY
```

### B1 — global model requalification

This was safe but too coarse.

The combined TQ2 calibration score falls below the threshold, so it rejects the **entire structured specialist artifact**.

Result:

```text
4 / 6 correct
0 unsafe qualification
2 over-rejections
```

It correctly blocks cross but unnecessarily throws away perfect circle and XOR transfer.

This is the opposite failure mode:

```text
ONE SUBREGIME FAILED
→ WHOLE MODEL FAMILY INVALID
```

also isn't lawful.

### C1 — task/regime-scoped

```text
6 / 6 correct
0 unsafe qualification
0 over-rejection
```

That is the surviving relation.

Not:

```text
MODEL M IS QUALIFIED
```

but something closer to:

```text
QUALIFIED(
    specialist,
    task/query,
    regime,
    qualification evidence,
    threshold,
    currentness
)
```

That is a substantial evolution of the specialist ABI.

---

# This changes the shape of Microseed again

Before these two cycles, we had:

```text
learned specialist
       ↓
MODEL_OUTPUT_ONLY
       ↓
semantic kernel
```

After Cycle 1:

```text
learned specialist
       ↓
MODEL_OUTPUT
       │
       ▼
REGIME AUTHORITY
       │
       ├── OOD → REFUSE
       │
       └── qualified
               ↓
       SELECTIVE SUPPORT
               ↓
          accept / defer
```

After Cycle 2:

```text
SPECIALIST IDENTITY
       ≠
CAPABILITY AUTHORITY

qualification is:

specialist
× task/query
× regime
× evidence
× threshold/currentness
```

So the current architecture is moving toward:

```text
                    QUERY
                      │
                      ▼
             REQUIRED CAPABILITY
                      │
                      ▼
       QUALIFIED SPECIALIST REGISTRY
                      │
        ┌─────────────┴─────────────┐
        │                           │
 TQ2 structured               INT8 specialist
 111 B model-state             366 B model-state
        │                           │
 circle/xor qualified           circle/cross/xor
 on this expanded regime         qualified
        │                           │
        └─────────────┬─────────────┘
                      ▼
                EXECUTIVE
                      │
                MODEL_OUTPUT
                      │
             semantic/warrant
                boundaries
```

And **that directly created the next frontier**.

# Evolution across the pair

The progression is clean:

```text
E09 / TQ2
learned specialists can add compact capability
        ↓
but model output has no direct authority

CYCLE 1 — OOD
confidence cannot tell us whether
the model is entitled to operate
        ↓
add external regime qualification
        ↓
regime membership still does not mean
this particular answer is correct
        ↓
selective abstention becomes explicit

CYCLE 2 — TRANSFER
original regime can be expanded
only through new target evidence
        ↓
transfer is not model-global
        ↓
qualification becomes task/query/regime scoped
```

That's a meaningful architectural evolution.

And it exposes something that I think is now becoming one of Microseed's central computational tricks:

> **Use the smallest mechanism whose capability is actually qualified for this exact problem, rather than asking one general model to own the whole cognitive surface.**

Because we now have a concrete asymmetry:

```text
TQ2 structured+bias
111 bytes
excellent where qualified
fails expanded cross

INT8 MLP
366 bytes
larger
but robust across all three tested expanded regimes
```

That naturally creates the next dragon.

# Selected frontier after the second hard stop

**`ATTN-E09-QUALIFIED-SPECIALIST-CASCADE`**

The next experiment should ask:

> Can the executive select the **smallest currently qualified specialist** for a query/regime, then fall back to the stronger int8 specialist only where the cheap specialist is unqualified—without loading both unnecessarily or laundering “fallback available” into authority?

The obvious boring candidate architecture is now:

```text
QUERY
  │
  ▼
qualification registry
  │
  ├── cheap specialist qualified?
  │        │
  │       YES → use cheap specialist
  │
  └────── NO
           │
           ▼
     stronger specialist qualified?
           │
       ┌───┴───┐
      YES      NO
       │        │
      use    UNKNOWN /
     fallback   refuse
```

That would let us test:

```text
resident bytes
cold-load bytes
selection cost
accuracy
abstention
currentness
fallback frequency
```

before inventing a learned router.

And we finally have enough evidence to make that experiment nontrivial: the two specialist families are **differently qualified in reality**, rather than artificially assigned roles.

**Second hard stop is intact. The cascade slice has not started.**

Two-cycle run complete. Both cycles reached their own hard stop, and **Cycle 2 was repaired to inherit the literal sealed Cycle 1 checkpoint before final handoff**, not the pre-seal candidate tree.
